### FIX01-CATCHUP-REQUEST-SCOPE-20261023 — private C4 catch-up response race correction
- **Role/scope:** Current user explicitly assigns this session to error fixing while DEV-01 independently develops features. Recovered `Development` at `f1e7f1140f0a7a91f14b873d591cbd0ed5b523f8`; read `docs/project-control/CHAT_CONTINUITY.md`, `NEXT_ACTION.md`, Phase 4 state, FIX-01 local memory/worklog, latest commits, C2/C4 tests and exact catch-up UI. Do not change any Markdown except this append-only `worklog.md`; do not poll CI/Vercel or touch OPS deployments, provider, DB schema or background registration.
- **Independently reproducible defect:** `apps/web/src/app/catch-up/catch-up-client.tsx` reset selected agent data in a post-render `useEffect` but left already-running async POST selection, GET detail, GET sources, POST review, refresh and pagination handlers unfenced. After switching agent A→B, an A response could update `manifest`, `items`, `sources` and `reviewedItemIds` for the B screen, or an old `finally` could clear B's busy state. Rapid same-tick repeated clicks could double-create selections because `busy` updates asynchronously. Editing mailbox/date after a selection left old scope results onscreen. This is a client-side privacy/context integrity and correctness error, not proof of server-side cross-tenant SQL bypass.
- **Test-first reproduction:** `b322143e85db66281db1bd0cf3bebfa19ae94ae2` introduced five native Node test cases for synchronous scoped request tokens (agent A→B, A→B→A, double-click and old-finally, invalid/same agent, UI source contract). The existing unfenced UI could not satisfy the request-scope assertions. `3494a3b97a3a6fd180c93644e5ad0d2e0ee55e2a` adds `apps/web/src/app/catch-up/request-scope.ts`: monotonic agent epoch and immediate in-flight guard. `b0219ef6b96ec1d4f04d0df7d5f107213be59999` applies it across selected-agent change, selection, detail, paged links, manual refresh and explicit review. Changed agents invalidate old results and never allow an earlier completion to release the new lock. `dac96e13cb18c61f6263b8a4628641644a34eda1` also invalidates old displays on mailbox/date edits and disables those edits while busy. `34d08b094e309f63fbea5dc7c2a3aad89e1427bf`/`b6af9e5e3b0cfb6e03a1b5dd73f482aaea0b4aeb` tighten regression source assertions.
- **Verification evidence:** Four independent Node v22 native behavioral cases on a stand-alone exact logic mirror PASS (4/4); isolated helper `tsc --strict --noEmit --target ES2022` completed successfully. Re-fetched final GitHub UI, helper, regression file and existing `briefing-c2-c4-contract.test.ts`: **12/12 targeted GitHub source invariants PASS** for old-response invalidation, A→B→A identity, double-operation lock, all four async paths, result reset, and preservation of existing source-linked review UI. This is *not* a native execution of the committed five-case repository test; repository dependencies and checkout unavailable locally (shell GitHub DNS fails). Web Vitest/tsc, React browser, real PostgreSQL, source-grant A/B negative, provider and production all NOT RUN. No feature flag, worker, routine, user permission, mailbox data or dispatch modified.
- **Lessons:** A frontend agent switch must synchronously invalidate pending response ownership, not rely on a post-render effect; monotonic revisions prevent A→B→A stale results. State-only `busy` guards are insufficient for same-tick concurrent requests; use a ref-like synchronous lock and fence old `finally`. Editing a mailbox/window must clear the previous manifest to avoid displaying mismatched scope data. Host tenancy/user grants remain separate hard boundaries.
- **Next precise FIX-01 action:** On sanitized dependency-equipped fresh `Development` record SHA then run `node --experimental-strip-types --test apps/web/tests/catch-up-request-scope.test.mjs`, `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web exec vitest run tests/briefing-c2-c4-contract.test.ts tests/briefing-access-route.test.ts tests/briefing-access.test.ts` and `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web typecheck`. ### FIX01-EXACT-ORIGIN-MAIL-AGENT-WRITES-20261023 — 2026-10-23 — completed source correction
- **Scope / owner instruction:** Acting as FIX-01/Sentinel source-error fixer, with DEV-01 independently developing features and OPS-01 responsible for CI/Vercel, host and provider/deployment. Started direct `Development` at `f788f7311468c51fd532b9327828afa2019425dd`. Read `docs/project-control/CHAT_CONTINUITY.md`, `NEXT_ACTION.md`, `CURRENT_PHASE.md`, `PROJECT_STATE.md`, `ISSUES.md`, FIX-01 role-local memory and append-only worklog. No other Markdown modified. Do not conflate feature source readiness with phase completion; Phase 4 remains in progress.
- **Reproducible source defect and RED:** `assertSameOrigin` in `apps/web/src/lib/identity/local-auth.ts` explicitly accepted any `origin.endsWith(".vercel.app")` when `APP_ORIGIN` ended in `.vercel.app`. Unrelated Vercel projects are different principals and cannot be trusted merely because they share a hosted domain suffix. In addition, authenticated draft creation/update/delete/sync, mail approval requests/decisions, read/move/archive mutations, chat tool-loop and compose POST, agent policy/mailbox bindings/deploy/settings/CRUD/version and conversation creation lacked explicit origin checks. All require independently valid actor/organization permissions, but the presence of credentials does not substitute for request-origin validation. Before the fix, the blanket suffix branch and missing endpoint calls were visibly present in source; test-first regression at `9ebb65b3` specifies disallowed Vercel sibling and required route gates. No live cross-origin exploitation claimed.
- **Full remedy and commits:** `8e2ddedceb44b9282ac3d045be5ed3c458415448` adds dependency-free `isTrustedRequestOrigin`: accept canonical serialized HTTP(S) origins only if they exactly equal the configured `APP_ORIGIN` or this request's real Host/protocol; reject sibling Vercel deployments, malformed/userinfo/path/port and invalid protocol. `5971319e3917bc1b5ef9059c89beac71e87438fd` replaces the shared broad suffix branch. Then exact-origin checks (403 `invalid_origin`, before DB setup) were added at `659806be` (chat compose), `a57a8356` (mail action request/approval), `ac248b99` (chat host-tool stream), `38feb1c5` (drafts POST/PATCH/DELETE), `2999abc1` (provider draft sync), `bedbf9ce` (read-state), `b956a6b4` (move/archive); and `437c9905` (agent create), `a3913be7` (agent edit/delete), `702c612e` (AI settings), `6c8075fb` (agent mailbox/channel binds), `79b94f15` (agent policy), `ef86df45` (agent deploy), `c01e0fcf` (agent versions), `4becbd30` (new chat). Updated the regression source at `45c50d0a` and `50b2edb2`. **19/19 named mutation handlers** now explicitly enforce the same shared origin policy; existing signed-in permissions, authorization checks, idempotency, human approval and provider delivery separation preserved. No permissions granted, data mutated or external mail sent.
- **Evidence (be precise):** Four local Node v22 native behavior tests on an isolated source mirror of the pure origin helper passed (4/4): exact APP_ORIGIN/routed Host acceptance, disallowed sibling Vercel and malformed origins, unexpected protocols/hosts, absent-Origin backward compatibility. Stand-alone strict TypeScript check on the mirrored helper exited zero. Exact latest GitHub Development blobs re-fetched: **19/19 mutation-handler source invariants pass** (403 + guard before `ensureDevSeed`, retained authorization helper) and five guard-contract source invariants pass; the committed regression file contains nine Node tests. **The full committed test suite, repo typechecks, authenticated browser, live server, CI/Vercel and deployed SHA were NOT RUN** because the available shell cannot resolve github.com and no dependency-equipped checkout/pnpm is mounted. Source/static evidence is not production security acceptance.
- **Boundary/limitations:** Missing Origin remains permitted for legitimate non-browser clients to preserve existing API behavior, while all requests still require authentication, org scoping and relevant permissions; this is a defensive exact-Origin comparison, **not** a claim of complete CSRF protection against Origin-omitting clients, credential compromise or hostile on-site scripts. A different Vercel project must never be first-party by suffix; exact Host fallback allows aliases actually serving this request. Do not allow `*.vercel.app` or a user-provided Host prefix as a blanket grant. Explicit Origin `null` is rejected.
- **Exact next FIX-01 runtime checks when a clean checkout is available:** `node --experimental-strip-types --test apps/web/tests/request-origin-write-boundary.test.mjs`; `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web exec vitest run tests/chat-authorization.test.ts tests/chat-conversation-history.test.ts tests/chat-ambient-context-policy.test.ts tests/email-action-idempotency-authorization.test.ts tests/briefing-access-route.test.ts`; `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web typecheck`. In an authenticated disposable browser, test same-host configured APP_ORIGIN/preview alias, a genuinely foreign `.vercel.app` Origin denied before mail drafts, approvals and admin agent-policy writes, missing-Origin server clients permitted only after auth, no unauthorized approval/dispatch; retain immutable audit and exact SHA. ### FIX01-DRAFT-NOOP-MESSAGE-READ-20261023 — 2026-10-23 — second independent source correction
- **Follow-on:** After completing FIX01-EXACT-ORIGIN-MAIL-AGENT-WRITES-20261023, continued independently reproducible source audit on direct `Development`, without waiting for CI/Vercel or changing any Markdown besides this append-only `worklog.md`. Concurrent DEV-01 product development and OPS-01 infrastructure/production remain untouched.
- **RED and root cause:** `packages/db/src/email/repository.ts` `updateLocalDraftForOrganization` accumulated editable column sets only when arrays or strings were supplied; for a PATCH with no usable editable field it directly returned `getMailMessageForOrganization(organizationId,messageId)`. Thus `PATCH /api/mail/drafts` with merely `organizationId` and an arbitrary ordinary `messageId` passed actor `email.write`, performed a generic organization-scoped message lookup and returned a mail-message object even though no draft was updated. The endpoint did not require `email.read`. This is an unintended read/write permission boundary and false `email.draft.updated` audit, not a claim of cross-organization SQL access. Source defect was directly reproduced by inspecting the zero-column branch, without touching a real mailbox.
- **Complete correction:** `d0bec992662e1e25ce3867cf6e359ba1fcb1fbb1` adds regression `apps/web/tests/mail-draft-noop-privacy.test.mjs` (empty/wrong-type versus explicit editable patch cases, API short circuit and DB non-draft isolation). `d3d089e67ea8beeae6e48068fc0504bcf94ba449` adds pure typed `hasDraftChanges` validator. `2b806c4beb959f4541bc7b355be5bdc2951430e3` rejects no-op direct draft PATCH with HTTP 422 `no_draft_changes` before DB message lookup. `fd53ad8ff1db83b119c4f3371eb2038a8ecedef2` changes repository zero-column branch to `return null`, never generic `getMailMessageForOrganization`. Real updates continue under existing tenant-scoped `m.is_draft = true`, `email.write`, approved-action lock, same-origin check and immutable audit. No message or external send operation invoked.
- **Verification:** Exact GitHub Development source re-fetch confirmed **8/8 targeted structural invariants PASS** (including scoped SQL, early 422 and retained origin guard). An isolated mirror of the pure helper passed **2/2 local native Node v22 tests** for no-op/wrong-typed input and actual editable values; stand-alone strict helper TypeScript check exited zero. **NOT RUN:** the newly committed full Node regression file, full web/db typechecks, Postgres tenant/actor negative integration, authenticated browser, CI/Vercel or production. Isolated mirror is not a package or runtime test.
- **Lesson:** A mutation repository must never silently become a general read endpoint when it has no columns to modify. Distinguish permissions for writing drafts versus reading mail, reject unsupported no-op payloads before looking up an arbitrary ID, and retain DB-level draft predicate even if the API validates.
- **Next exact FIX-01 checks:** On a sanitized current `Development` checkout run `node --experimental-strip-types --test apps/web/tests/mail-draft-noop-privacy.test.mjs apps/web/tests/request-origin-write-boundary.test.mjs`, `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web exec vitest run tests/mail-drafts-write-authorization.test.mjs tests/email-action-idempotency-authorization.test.ts` where applicable to runner, and `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web typecheck && pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/db typecheck`. In disposable tenant DB, verify email.write-only (no email.read) actor's PATCH on non-draft email ID with empty fields returns 422 without message contents/audit mutation, valid draft change works, wrong-organization IDs fail, and approved drafts remain locked; verify foreign Vercel Origin 403 and true same-origin flows. No CI/Vercel polling, no human approval bypass. More independent FIX-01 source errors may be addressed; DEV-01 features and OPS-01 live deployment unchanged.

Preserved folder PATCH `organization.update`, draft POST/PATCH/DELETE `email.write`, send approval `organization.update`.

Default context remains the authenticated current organization. Avoid prematurely changing session ownership or grants.

### FIX01-ATOMIC-MAIL-FOLDER-BATCH-20261023 — archive/move partial-success repair
- **Scope/recovery:** Continued as FIX-01/Sentinel on direct `Development` from `e60b488c24c55d6f2c2be075650d422a2d1ba20c`; read exact `docs/project-control/CHAT_CONTINUITY.md`, `NEXT_ACTION.md`, PROJECT_STATE/CURRENT_PHASE, FIX-01 prior memory/issues and latest worklog. Phase 4 remains in progress. Owner explicitly delegates only source-error repair while DEV-01 develops features, prohibits any other Markdown edits, and says not to wait/poll GitHub Actions or Vercel.
- **Source RED/root cause:** `PATCH /api/mail/messages/folder` attempted to archive/move all requested IDs, then returned a 404 if the database updated fewer rows than `messageIds.length`. Original `archiveMailMessagesForOrganization`/`moveMailMessagesForOrganization` each ran a single unconstrained subset `UPDATE ... WHERE m.id = ANY(...)`; inaccessible IDs, a missing archive folder on one of several mailboxes, cross-account target folders and duplicate submitted IDs could therefore mutate some mail while the API returned a failure. The UI could report an error despite irreversible partial mailbox state changes. The endpoint silently filtered nonstring IDs and had no strict UUID/duplicate/batch-size validation. Reproduced directly from read-only source and existing static regression assertion; no real email or DB modified.
- **Complete RED→GREEN correction:** Added `apps/web/tests/mail-folder-batch-atomic.test.mjs` first (`ac2fcfb71d546f85841c6d342f42992720a04b99`) covering malformed, duplicate/case-collision IDs, exact account membership, shared transaction, locked rows/destinations, all-or-nothing and exported functions. Added pure `apps/web/src/lib/mail/folder-batch-validation.ts` (`fe524b1a68540c2a104cc03df87056417a3d1fb0`) to enforce 1–100 complete/unique UUID IDs and valid target UUIDs without dropping inputs. Replaced both DB helpers via `changeMailFoldersAtomically` (`d8f193e8f969e4b93978387db3eb5823813405de`): in one `withTransaction`, lock tenant + live-account scoped selected messages `FOR UPDATE`, verify exact cardinality, lock real tenant archive/move folders, demand exactly one archive folder per included mailbox or a single matching move-account destination, update all rows using prevalidated per-account folder IDs, and **throw/roll back** if the update count is inconsistent. Invalid mixed sets return 0 before any updates. ### FIX01-ATOMIC-MAIL-READ-STATE-20261023 — mixed-ID read/unread partial update correction
- **Scope:** Follow-on independent FIX-01 mailbox bulk-operation defect corrected after `FIX01-ATOMIC-MAIL-FOLDER-BATCH-20261023`, direct `Development`; preserved owner instruction that only source files/tests may change and root `worklog.md` is the sole mutable Markdown. No CI/Vercel waits, feature development, provider mail operations, user permission changes, schema changes or production deployment.
- **Reproducible defect/RED:** `PATCH /api/mail/messages/read-state` accepts up to 100 message IDs but silently filtered invalid values, allowed UUID case-duplicate IDs and invoked `updateMailReadStateForOrganization` that updated only accessible tenant/account rows. It reported `{updated:N}` and wrote a successful read-state audit even if some requested messages were missing, outside the org or inaccessible due to deleted accounts. That falsely presents a successful bulk operation while changing a partial subset. Direct read-only source inspection; no customer mailbox accessed.
- **Complete fix:** Regression `apps/web/tests/mail-read-state-batch-atomic.test.mjs` first (`8c5fe9ff0bc50bb6084e1f0b31d6646975863e1b`) adds native Node cases for strict IDs, route audit order and transaction/rollback SQL. `0bbdd1c0f70ed8e0426cfefba1dab0553d09c865` shares the existing strict UUID batch helper as `isValidMailMessageIds`. `52494a5376ec12bf91f224f7a6da9cfdd0715f8a` replaces subset SQL with one tenant-scoped `withTransaction` that locks all requested message rows and source accounts, requires the entire set to exist, updates once, rolls back if the affected count disagrees. `1ea3add5f81222da45e4fcc577dbaaafaf1c1eb3` corrects selected-row SQL to contiguous `$1/$2` bind parameters (not an unused `$1` or mismatched `$2/$3`). `98fe285cdf0a0a9ec283ef61a47a24c1c38bdb5f` replaces silent route filtering with full batch validation; 404 if exact set unavailable, and only **after complete success** records read/unread audit. `e668af0a1f64bc5ec051112d24f04e5a1140af6f` pins safe contiguous SQL placeholders in regression. Existing email.read authorization, org scoping, 100-ID limit, first-party origin and UI single-message functionality preserved.
- **Verification:** Re-fetched all current GitHub blob sources for the two atomic bulk fixes plus both new regressions, and the adjusted existing Vitest contract: **14/14 targeted integrated structural invariants PASS** including SQL bind numbers, read-state audit order, mutex-style row locks and matching account/tenant constraints. Native Node 22 isolated mirror of the pure batch validator **4/4 passed**, and stand-alone strict `tsc --noEmit` on that mirror exited 0. **NOT RUN** actual committed Node regression files, PostgreSQL transactional behavior, repo typechecks/Vitest, authorized browser, GitHub Actions/Vercel, production. No claims of live rollback without a disposable DB.
- **Lessons:** Reading and changing read-state is a mailbox mutation: a set of 100 requested IDs means all 100 or none, unless partial semantics are *explicitly* requested. Audits must follow confirmed complete database writes. Silently filtering malformed IDs and returning the count of the surviving subset misleads callers. SQL selected-row queries must use contiguous parameters rather than pass unused extra bind values.
- **Next on sanitized equipped Development:** `node --experimental-strip-types --test apps/web/tests/mail-folder-batch-atomic.test.mjs apps/web/tests/mail-read-state-batch-atomic.test.mjs`; `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web exec vitest run tests/mail-folder-mutation-contract.test.ts tests/email-repository-mail-contract.test.ts`; `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/db typecheck && pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web typecheck`. Disposable Postgres negative: valid+cross-org IDs should change zero folder and zero read state, incomplete/duplicate sets should return failure without success audit; positive: exact unique folder and read-state batch should update all; race source account deletion and archive absence. Preserve DEV-01 product and OPS-01 release/provider ownership; do not wait for CI/Vercel.

### FIX01-WORKER-MAIL-FOLDER-TS2532-20261023 — worker Docker build compile repair

- **User-reported RED (actual build, Development):** `docker compose` reached `RUN pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/worker build` and TypeScript failed with `packages/db/src/email/repository.ts(438,11): TS2532 Object is possibly 'undefined'`. The concurrent web image build was **canceled**, not independently reported as failing. The underlying TS config has `strict: true` and `noUncheckedIndexedAccess: true`; Docker only copied the code and ran pnpm installation before the compiler error.
- **Recovery/ownership:** Read current `Development` SHA `4d19cf41749ea0c16c60d0c6a6a812ad0d94c1c2`, mandatory `docs/project-control/CHAT_CONTINUITY.md` and `NEXT_ACTION.md`, prior atomic-folder source/test and FIX-01 worklog. Owner requests **only error repair** concurrent with DEV-01 feature work, no changes to Markdown except this append-only `worklog.md`, no gate/CI/Vercel waiting. Phase 4 remains in progress; OPS-01 controls deployment.
- **Precise defect:** Newly introduced atomic mail-folder move helper first checked `destination.rows.length !== 1`, then directly accessed `destination.rows[0].email_account_id`. Array length tests do not narrow an indexed item when `noUncheckedIndexedAccess` is enabled, so TS2532 is correct. Blind non-null assertions would hide the optional-result condition rather than preserve the fail-closed policy.
- **Complete repair:** `143e09c70abd3fa06599254500fd15435c45955b` adds a focused RED regression in `apps/web/tests/mail-folder-batch-atomic.test.mjs` requiring explicit narrowing. `6d8278a52505ee8c116329d05ef68dc296d0b8de` now assigns `const destinationRow = destination.rows[0]` and fails closed on `!destinationRow`, wrong destination cardinality, multiple source accounts or mismatched account before using `destinationRow.email_account_id`; carries `targets = [destinationRow]` into the existing transaction. Original tenant account restrictions, exact row locks, multi-source archive and rollback-on-partial-update are unchanged. `011b8cb85cb19473831cba6d4a40dc890f7bf2d2` updates the previous Vitest folder contract to test the narrowed row rather than the unsafe expression.
- **Verification:** A local isolated TypeScript reproduction under actual available `tsc 5.8.3 --strict --noUncheckedIndexedAccess --noEmit --target es2022` confirmed RED **TS2532** for the original indexed access and GREEN **exit 0** for the exact narrowed pattern; source re-fetched from the latest GitHub branch passed **7/7 target structural checks** for narrowing, fail-closed destination logic and unchanged all-or-nothing behavior. The full repository `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/worker build`, `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web build`, TypeScript of all packages, committed regression runner and real PostgreSQL transaction integration were **NOT RUN**: this assistant's container does not have an accessible clone or repository dependencies, and GitHub DNS is unavailable to its shell. The user-provided previous worker failure is the verified baseline, not a post-fix build result. No customer data or provider operation accessed.
- **Lesson:** Under `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`, `rows.length === 1` does not prove `rows[0]` exists to TypeScript. Bind the row and test its presence explicitly; preserve runtime fail-closed behavior as well as compiler correctness. A canceled parallel web build is not evidence of a web build failure.
- **Exact next FIX-01 operator commands (do not wait/poll any gates):** On a fresh local `Development` checkout at the fixed SHA, run `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/worker build` immediately, then `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web build`, `node --experimental-strip-types --test apps/web/tests/mail-folder-batch-atomic.test.mjs`, and `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web exec vitest run tests/mail-folder-mutation-contract.test.ts`. If new concrete failures appear, route only source errors to FIX-01 and fix through full regression; OPS-01 controls Docker/production environment. In a disposable tenant DB validate missing move folder returns 0, cross-account move returns 0, valid same-account move updates all, and mixed archive batches stay atomic. Keep DEV-01's concurrent features intact.

### FIX01-EMAIL-COMPOSE-PROVIDER-CAPABILITY-REFRESH-20261023 — stale connected mailbox flags

- **User-reported symptom / security handling:** Read-only `GET /api/chat/mail-compose` for an existing agent returned two primary connected mailboxes with `canDraft=false` and `canRequestApproval=false`. Both reported `missing_email_write`; one also reported `provider_drafts_unsupported` and `provider_send_unsupported`. Agent policy UI showed 16 selected email tools, explicitly not yet active until saved. An authentication session cookie appeared in the pasted diagnostic: treat as compromised and rotate/revoke it; its bytes must never be included in code, logs, tests, tool output or persistent notes. No live mailboxes, user records, sessions or credentials were accessed by FIX-01.
- **Recovery and responsibility:** Re-read `docs/project-control/CHAT_CONTINUITY.md`, `NEXT_ACTION.md`, current GitHub Development head `5402dbb27636272fbfe8f2e13f4bd30f1ee8df79`, existing compose readiness/runtime, worker provider registration, IMAP/Gmail adapters, account repository and role permission definitions. Phase 4 and DEV-01 feature work remain independently gated. Owner permits only source bug fixes; append evidence only to root `worklog.md`, no other Markdown edits and no CI/Vercel waiting or live provider operations.
- **Authorization boundary is NOT a code error:** `email.write` is deliberately excluded from all `SYSTEM_ROLE_PERMISSIONS`. `PostgresIdentityRepository.getPermissionsForRole` derives permissions from those fixed role definitions. The UI agent tool selection only configures a separate saved agent policy; it cannot elevate signed-in actor rights. Do **not** silently add `email.write` to owner/admin/member defaults, self-grant via UI or bypass the host executor, approval requirement or provider capability checks. Any permission-grant change needs explicit owner/security authorization and separate policy/audit design. Even after provider fix, read-only readiness correctly stays false until `email.write` is intentionally granted.
- **Distinct source defect reproduced:** `apps/web/src/app/api/email-accounts/route.ts` seeded new IMAP/SMTP account capabilities with conservative `drafts:false,send:false` while status was pending, then its `reconnect_imap_smtp` branch changed the saved account directly to `connected` after only IMAP/SMTP socket verification; it never performed capability discovery. The **regular** connected/pending/error mailbox sync worker `apps/worker/src/email/mailbox-sync-worker.ts` likewise called `adapter.connect`, synchronized messages and marked account `connected` with `updateSyncState`, but never called `adapter.discoverCapabilities` or persisted its `CapabilitySet`. A separate utility `discoverEmailAccountStructure` performs discovery but was not used in the main sync. Therefore one or more successful working mailboxes can remain forever with stale false draft/send support. Both Gmail and IMAP adapters implement their own actual discovery.
- **Complete targeted correction (tests first):** `661df7b387355ddcad8be5fd3c2d7f77ac7ce296` adds five-case `apps/web/tests/email-sync-capability-refresh.test.mjs` regression (normal sync discovery order and persistence, no guessed provider grants, reconnect pending until verified, honest UI, independent actor permission). `b53c0993bff7ec4eb6cd3215a47fd16fe7033dde` updates all **three** mailbox worker SQL candidate projections (direct queue, producer and poller) to include actual stored capability flags, then after the live provider connects normalizes `adapter.discoverCapabilities()` and updates account capabilities only if a capability changed, **before** the normal successful-sync transition sets status `connected`. No send, approval, or role change; unsupported flags remain false. `0383b4fa431ab5a6b3aacf83f083e1ce266a22d1` changes socket-tested IMAP/SMTP reconnection to status `pending`, with `discoveryPending:true` and audit metadata `pending_worker_verification`; it is prioritized by the existing worker and cannot prematurely claim capabilities verified. `45cc5308c307ba2b30d18005a125a627e9c813d5` updates mailbox UI to say capability discovery is pending rather than falsely restored. `de4f5a8bfc126c079851d771f8eecd26e055b25f` corrects the regression's admin role assertion to inspect the full parent POST scope.
- **Verification and limitations:** Re-fetched exact current GitHub blobs for worker, API, UI, compose, test, shared types and provider adapters; **17/17 targeted source invariants PASS**, including three updated worker candidate queries, discovery after connection and before status promotion, idempotent DB snapshot refresh, pending reconnect, preserved role authorization and read-only `email.write` preflight. Five new native Node test cases committed but actual native test runner, worker/web full builds, real PostgreSQL, mailbox provider capability probe, OAuth scope acceptance, production rollout and CI/Vercel **NOT RUN** in connector-only environment; no live capability claims. The owner-supplied response could also reflect genuinely unsupported provider functions or an old deployment; do not promise that the source patch alone makes mail compose eligible.
- **Next exact FIX-01 verification on dependency-equipped latest Development SHA:** `node --experimental-strip-types --test apps/web/tests/email-sync-capability-refresh.test.mjs`; `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/worker build`; `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web build`; `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web exec vitest run tests/email-accounts-lifecycle.test.ts tests/email-capabilities.test.ts tests/email-write-permission-contract.test.ts tests/chat-mail-compose-readiness.test.mjs` where runner supports. ### FIX01-CHAT-FIRST-LOAD-DRAFT-ISOLATION-20261023 — FIX-01/Sentinel, Development
- **Authorization/scope:** Owner instructed error-fixing only on `Rilan-Dev/ai-comms-agent/tree/Development`, no changes to any Markdown except append-only root `worklog.md`, no waiting on CI/Vercel or acceptance gates. At start the current GitHub HEAD was `10860d93c4fc2aac299ae3ae4132d8bd235ad7e8`. DEV-01 retains feature delivery; OPS-01 owns environment/CI/deployment.
- **Reported CLI symptom and disposition:** Owner's previous host snippet shows a truncated command (`pnpm --filter @ai-com`) followed by `ERROR Unknown option: 'recursive'`. It does not contain a complete command or a reproducible application stack trace, and does NOT justify changing package scripts, lockfile, pnpm version or application code. `package.json` still declares `pnpm@12.8.1`. Use full `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web exec vitest run ...` or `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web typecheck` commands and route genuine host tooling errors to OPS-01.
- **Independent source defect 1 (RED, precise trace):** `getOrCreateChatConversation` used two statements, `SELECT most recent active chat` followed by `INSERT`, inside a normal transaction but without any serialization. With two concurrent first-load requests for a new org+agent (two tabs, chat and voice), both could read no row and insert two different automatically created active chat sessions; a simultaneous explicit New Chat POST could also race the same SELECT and select unexpected history. The schema intentionally permits multiple active chats (no uniqueness on agent+channel), so introducing a global unique constraint or deleting duplicates is invalid. This is a real transaction interleaving proven from source; no actual production reproduction or SQL integration was available.
- **Defect 1 remedy:** Regression-before-fix `7c05d438fc7440927ae875c91435c2042a4fb7b8` adds four focused Vitest SQL-order/tenant/behavior contracts. Fix `d3b002a163c067747dcfdad9c7e442814a1bb2ec` obtains a transaction-scoped `SELECT id FROM agents WHERE organization_id=$1 AND id=$2 FOR UPDATE` row lock **before** the active conversation SELECT, checks scoped agent existence, and makes deliberate `createChatConversation` acquire the same lock before inserting. Same-org same-agent requests serialize; different accounts/orgs do not contend on one global lock; intentional many-chat history remains supported. Added deterministic mocked-transaction Vitest `cc578587b5c6dc71b7a7490437c1d224bc7ca545` covering concurrent first loads, separate user-created chat, tenant separation and simultaneous new-chat operation. No migration, DB record deletion, queue action, external provider or automated feature activation.
- **Independent source defect 2 (RED, precise trace):** `ChatClient.switchAgent` had client/response-context fencing but no draft guard. Switching agents while the prompt textarea contained unsent content silently retained the old draft, allowing accidental submission of the previous agent's prompt to a different agent. The new-chat action already asked before discarding a draft; agent switch omitted consent and proper isolation. No production data accessed.
- **Defect 2 remedy:** Regression-before-fix `56c845f6d860121ab5cfcf187a0dfcd4a42dd563` adds a context-race case that requires confirmation before the network fetch and clearing only on committed successful agent switch. Source fix `2d82e808da3fc2e4a9c620351ba878bc28b6da49` asks `Switch agents and discard your unsent message?`, returns unchanged when denied, clears draft after a verified successful agent/context switch and preserves it on failure; previous async epoch fencing and compose-review invalidation untouched.
- **Verification evidence and limits:** Re-fetched six source/test files plus package metadata on current `Development` `cc578587b5c6dc71b7a7490437c1d224bc7ca545` and evaluated **16/16 focused source-level invariants PASS** for scoped locking and statement order, preserved manual New Chat semantics, new 4 + 4 regression cases, mock transaction overlap, draft consent and failure preservation. Native Vitest, `pnpm`/TypeScript, PostgreSQL concurrent behavior, browser navigation and actual production acceptance **NOT RUN** in GitHub connector environment. Source checks and mocked transaction tests existing in source are not equivalent to executed regression tests; no downstream green claim.
- **Failed/unsafe approaches avoided:** Do not ignore a non-unique SELECT-then-INSERT race, globally lock the conversations table, impose a unique active-chat constraint conflicting with the owner's multi-session feature, delete older sessions, relax org/agent predicates, reset drafts before server switch success, silently send prompts to the newly selected agent, or relabel an incomplete pnpm command as a source bug. Preserve user choice and cross-tenant boundaries.
- **Prevention:** Multi-step get-or-create requires per-owner serialization inside a single DB transaction, and explicit creates must follow the same lock order. A user draft is *agent-context-sensitive*: changing agent identity requires consent and an atomic successful context reset; network failures must not lose user text.
- **Exact next FIX-01 check on equipped checkout:** `git switch Development && git pull --ff-only origin Development`; `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web exec vitest run tests/chat-conversation-first-load-race.test.ts tests/chat-conversation-db-concurrency.test.ts tests/chat-agent-context-race.test.ts tests/chat-conversation-history.test.ts`; `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/db typecheck && pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web typecheck`. With a disposable PostgreSQL fixture test simultaneous first GET on an agent with no chats, explicit New Chat, and existing session history, plus browser canceled/failed/successful agent switch with unsent text. Report exact SHA/result and route host pnpm errors to OPS-01; DEV-01 continues product work without waiting.

It closes the stream and does not claim a provider action failed or succeeded, because post-tool failures may be ambiguous. Unrelated model/provider, agent grants and routing stay unchanged.

No `console.*` remains in the chat route; shared authorization only logs validated five-character SQLSTATE for unexpected errors. The added Vitest regression and successful response contract are present in source. The 3/8 vs 11/11 comparison is *static/source verification*, not a native test execution.

No claims of production remediation or verified email delivery. OPS-01 handles any affected production logs, approved deploy and redacted environmental incident response.
- **Failed/unsafe options rejected:** Do not preserve raw `error.message` in SSE for debugging, log complete error objects that may contain secrets, silently turn valid 401/403 into 500, synthesize SSE `done` on failure, retry uncertain side effects automatically, grant email.write, or modify DEV-01 product features.
- **Prevention/next:** Treat both HTTP error bodies and SSE event frames as externally observable API surfaces; reuse centralized safe error classification, and keep operational logging metadata-only. Next equipped Development validation: `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web exec vitest run tests/chat-stream-error-boundary.test.ts tests/chat-authorization.test.ts tests/streaming-conversations.test.ts` plus `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web typecheck`; mock unexpected errors carrying sensitive strings to confirm no user-visible or logged exposure. ## FIX01-MANUAL-COMPOSE-REVIEW-PERSISTED-SNAPSHOT-20261023 — FIX-01 / Sentinel — Development, 2026-10-23

- **Authorization and recovered source:** Owner asked to continue independent substantial bug fixes directly on `Development` while DEV-01 develops features, without waiting for GitHub Actions, Vercel, gate passage or continuous polling. Recovered current Development HEAD `67e69b9d3a4c3dc46bfd2e5ccd531db1194d959f`, latest commits, read `docs/project-control/CHAT_CONTINUITY.md`, `NEXT_ACTION.md`, central `AGENT_MEMORY.md`, `AGENT_BACKLOG.md`, FIX-01 role memory and root worklog. Phase 4 remains implementation_in_progress; other agent's concurrent RED test `67e69b9d` concerns unverified model-claimed email actions and is outside this independent composer fix. No other Markdown was edited; only this append to root lowercase `worklog.md`.
- **Independent reproduced defect / RED:** In `apps/web/src/app/mail/compose/compose-client.tsx`, editing a saved draft invoked a 900ms debounce `saveDraft`, but `requestSendApproval()` immediately POSTed the current `draftId` without waiting for that save or verifying content. The server built approval `recipient_snapshot` from the *persisted older draft*, so approval could be requested for old recipients/body even while the UI displayed changes. Multiple overlapping saves could race and the default mailbox load effect unconditionally set a first connected/pending mailbox after an existing draft's actual `email_account_id` had loaded. Existing mailbox IDs became inconsistent with the draft/approval and API could return misleading 404. The issue is a practical review-integrity error independent of provider credentials or CI.
- **RED regression and correction:** Added `apps/web/tests/manual-compose-review-integrity.test.ts` first at `98c0e05b48c0e642f07c15ad45ff859333115eb0`: exact canonical recipient/subject/body, malformed snapshot denial, no asynchronous autosave path, reviewed-snapshot save/approval fence, already-bound sender stability, and scoped backend 409 response. Added pure `apps/web/src/lib/mail/manual-compose-review.ts` at `e572ab7210256c05c89cea496a8077a2efb6d2f8` with strict normalized address arrays (To required; CC/BCC optional), nonblank subject, unchanged body, deterministic persisted-vs-reviewed equality. This is only an additional review-consistency contract; actor/organization/tool approval authorization remains enforced separately.
- **Completed UI correction:** `apps/web/src/app/mail/compose/compose-client.tsx` `27ee707793cd7a147d376e02eb55b34f099f44cb` removes racing implicit 900ms autosave, requires an explicitly confirmed save of current editable To/CC/BCC/subject/body before the approval button enables, invalidates saved review state on every edit, disables edits during save and after approval, uses synchronous in-flight ref to deny double submit, requires the server returned draft ID/mailbox and exact persisted snapshot to match what was reviewed, requires the action response to explicitly report pending_approval before claiming success, and sends `expectedSnapshot` with the approval POST. Existing saved drafts retain their persisted sending mailbox; an asynchronous account-list load now uses `setAccountId(current => current || first?.id || "")` and only chooses connected defaults. Existing drafts cannot arbitrarily change their From mailbox; switching From requires a new draft. Failed load for a requested draft cannot silently become a fresh draft.
- **Completed host rejection:** `apps/web/src/app/api/mail/actions/route.ts` commit `c8cf43a03369d4bd9983d1ea57d734489da425ef` uses the pure `matchesManualComposeReview` on manual composer `expectedSnapshot` (only when supplied), compares it with the actual organization-scoped draft snapshot before calling `createEmailAction`, and returns `409 draft_review_changed` if recipient/subject/body have changed. This does NOT grant permission, create/send mail without approval, or break non-manual callers lacking the optional reviewed snapshot. Test expectation updated to include synchronous in-flight guard commit `990e18d3618ebcd7daf76b9225825280d546477f`.
- **Actually executed verification:** Fetched exact committed helper, suite, composer and action route source blobs. Executed the pure helper function bodies after erasing TS-only declarations, and all five committed Vitest-style test callbacks with a minimal assertion shim: **5/5 PASS**. Checked normalized email case, body/To/CC/subject changes, missing/malformed sender/recipient/subject data, UI save-before-approval/source fence, original draft sender selection, and server-side 409 predicate. This is **NOT** a native Vitest runner or TypeScript compiler, browser interaction, PostgreSQL, deployed environment or actual provider email send. No emails/approvals were executed.

### FIX01-CHAT-TURN-NAVIGATION-RACE-20261023 — FIX-01 / Sentinel — 2026-10-23
- **Authorization / recovery:** Owner requested FIX-01-only continued source error correction on `Rilan-Dev/ai-comms-agent` `Development`, independently of DEV-01 product implementation and OPS-01 CI/Vercel/deployment. Explicit owner restriction: read control docs but edit NO Markdown except root lowercase `worklog.md`. Started current GitHub `Development` at `3d9c3182b82cf731598cbaaae129aefe000d208a`; recovered `AGENTS.md`, `docs/project-control/CHAT_CONTINUITY.md`, `NEXT_ACTION.md`, `CURRENT_PHASE.md`, shared `AGENT_MEMORY.md`, role-local FIX-01 memory/worklog, central sessions/events, backlog, issues, verification and current Chat implementation. Phase 4 `implementation_in_progress`; new DEV-01 sidebar and multi-session functionality preserved. The owner's abbreviated `pnpm --filter @ai-com` / `Unknown option: 'recursive'` log does not identify a compiler/application source defect and was not treated as a license to change pnpm tooling.
- **Independently reproducible source race:** The new `ChatClient.startNewChat()` synchronously sets `newChatInFlightRef.current=true` before sending its POST, but `sendMessage()` checks only `newChatBusy` (React state updated asynchronously). Rapid keyboard Enter/Send can therefore submit `/api/chat/stream` with the **old** conversation ID between the New chat click and state repaint. Independently, two Enter/Send invocations in one rendering frame can both see `busy=false` and send duplicate turns. The Recent conversations link and agent-switch handler also checked rendered state, not a synchronous Chat-turn submission fence. Source path `apps/web/src/app/chat/chat-client.tsx`; live user race not claimed.
- **Root cause / failed approaches:** A state setter is not a synchronous mutex. Disabling buttons based only on `busy` or `newChatBusy` (or relying on low probability of rapid clicks) cannot prevent event handlers from firing before rerender. - **Independent confirmed email authorization mismatch:** In `apps/web/src/app/api/mail/actions/route.ts`, the POST route creating an approval request required `organization.update`, identical to the PATCH route that *decides* human approval. A user authorized to draft/request a send therefore could not request review from the manual composer unless they were also organization admin. Granting admin rights to work around it would violate separation-of-duty and previously recorded email.write RBAC requirements. Separately send_email could snapshot a draft with malformed/empty To/CC/BCC and forward it to approval only to fail at worker execution, or silently lose bad recipients through filtering.

Source route correction committed `48ffc37e632977b31ab6be52dd08382efa715f7b`: POST permission switched to `email.write` while PATCH and privileged GET retain `organization.update`. `validRecipients` now rejects comma/semicolon/angle bracket injection characters; for send_email, validates each persisted To/CC/BCC address and requires the filtered counts to match original arrays so bad recipients cannot be silently removed. No system role auto-grant and no direct email send or approval decision is introduced.

Cross-surface identity intent remains distinct: email.write lets a member ask for human review, organization.update allows the human reviewer to decide; missing/altered recipients reject source. ### FIX01-VOICE-TURN-ORIGIN-AND-SSE-ERROR-20261023 — complete voice host-tool security boundary

- **Role and authorization:** FIX-01 / Sentinel only, source error correction directly on `Development`. Resumed after the earlier chat/voice email-feedback source work: Chat natural-language send prompts now enter `/api/chat/stream` rather than silently opening `/api/chat/mail-compose`; the host returns honest signed-in `email.write` denial or verified draft/approval outcome without claiming that mail was sent. This work is a distinct follow-up defect, not additional DEV-01 functionality. Recovered current branch, `docs/project-control/CHAT_CONTINUITY.md`, `NEXT_ACTION.md`, shared AGENT_MEMORY, central AGENT_EVENTS/HANDOFF/ISSUES/VERIFICATION, FIX-01 role memory/worklog and relevant source. Phase 4 stays `implementation_in_progress`. Owner says leave all other Markdown untouched, append only root `worklog.md`, no CI/Vercel polling or production modification. Concurrent DEV-01 five-day catch-up/Overview work retained.
- **Symptom / source RED:** `POST /api/voice/turn` can process an authenticated voice turn, increment the session counter, persist an optional chat transcript and invoke the host email tool loop, but unlike the chat/mail mutation routes it had no `assertSameOrigin` check before those operations. A browser request with a foreign explicit Origin was therefore not rejected at the voice ingress. Its SSE `catch` also emitted `error.message` verbatim. Model/provider/SQL/tool errors can embed credentials, organization information, mail text, arguments or internal error detail; exposing raw exception text over a browser voice stream is unsafe. Authentication, active session ownership, trusted ToolContext, email.write, mailbox capability checks and human send approval were already present; none should be relaxed. No attack, live voice session, or provider send was attempted.
- **Regression first:** Added native Node regression `apps/web/tests/voice-turn-origin-error-boundary.test.mjs` at `83ea5018637fe992d07484a6f3ca108bf9ed0ca1`, with four focused tests: exact first-party vs foreign Vercel Origin policy, voice endpoint guard BEFORE `ensureDevSeed`/turn-count/host tools, no verbatim tool/provider errors in SSE, and unchanged host-verified tool result and saved chat answer. Initial test source had overescaped nested regex literals; corrected the test source immediately at `5df8f75d123a720331b4577eb4d73e2e5df3f89a` to use robust literal checks instead. This was a **test syntax issue**, not a production source error.
- **Complete smallest fix:** `d903b1413bc6e921933ebd27a03ed0aa9286b79e` imports the already-approved shared `assertSameOrigin` and runs it at the first line of the voice POST handler, returning `403 invalid_origin` for mismatched explicit browser origins before DB setup. Keeps existing missing-Origin compatibility and subsequent actor/session/capability/approval gates; does not create direct sends or change roles. Replaces the voice SSE raw `error.message` with a fixed safe failure message directing the user to review saved drafts and pending approvals before retry, since an interrupted tool call may have had a side effect before a stream failure. Reuses existing redacted `authorizationErrorToResponse` for outer JSON errors.
- **Source verification and limits:** Fresh GitHub `Development` blob re-fetch at `6b7846e50ddd78d79235cd9dca6e59543486c21b` passed **10/10 targeted source-contract checks**: early Origin fence/403, no raw voice SSE exception, preserved host tool and saved JSON/SSE answers, existing chat verified-tool fallback and independent actor write gate, valid four-case test content. Actual `node --experimental-strip-types --test apps/web/tests/voice-turn-origin-error-boundary.test.mjs`, web Vitest/build/typecheck, authenticated disposable voice browser, database and provider integration, and deployed SHA **NOT RUN** in this connector-only environment. Static checks do not prove runtime or production remediation. No email, WhatsApp or voice provider action performed.
- **Failed approaches and prevention:** Do not trust a browser referrer or presence of an authenticated cookie in place of exact Origin checking. Do not echo arbitrary exceptions to SSE consumers for debugging; classify internal errors, emit only safe user-facing text and preserve validated authorization status separately. Do not fix security by disabling voice tool execution or auto-granting `email.write`. A canceled stream is not evidence a draft/send request did not already occur; prompt the human to verify pending approvals before retrying.
- **Exact next FIX-01/OPS-01 split:** In a clean current `Development` checkout, run `node --experimental-strip-types --test apps/web/tests/voice-turn-origin-error-boundary.test.mjs apps/web/tests/chat-voice-email-action-feedback.test.mjs apps/web/tests/request-origin-write-boundary.test.mjs`, `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web exec vitest run tests/voice-turn-lifecycle-guard.test.ts tests/voice-email-tool-runtime.test.ts tests/chat-host-tool-loop.test.ts`, then `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web typecheck`. On a disposable signed-in voice session, verify valid same-origin voice turns still stream and persist trustworthy outcomes, foreign Origin rejects with 403 before any counted turn, and a simulated throw does not expose exception markers; no automatic send. OPS-01 owns deployed revision, environment, credential and CI/CD handling; DEV-01 owns new product capabilities. The previously pasted browser session cookie should be revoked/rotated through account operations; do not store or replay it.

### FIX01-PNPM-RECURSIVE-CLEAN-COLLISION-20261023 — 2026-10-23
- **Owner/authority:** FIX-01 / Sentinel on `Development`; owner requests continue independent error correction without CI/Vercel/review waits, never touch DEV-01's new features, and modify **no Markdown other than root `worklog.md`**. Recovered current `docs/project-control/CHAT_CONTINUITY.md`, `NEXT_ACTION.md`, shared agent memory, central session/event/handoff, FIX-01 memory/worklog, issues and verification; read-only all project-control docs. Phase 4 remains `implementation_in_progress`; DEV-01 is concurrently implementing five-day catch-up/chat UX; OPS-01 owns environment, deployment and pnpm binary installation.
- **User symptom / attribution:** The most recent operator paste fast-forwarded `Development` from `aefa20c9` to `de559b54`, then shows a truncated `pnpm --filter @ai-com...` command ending in `ERROR Unknown option: 'recursive'`. The complete operator command and pnpm binary provenance are **unknown**; this is not evidence that a root `clean` script actually ran.
- **Independently verified repo root cause:** Root `package.json` had `"clean": "pnpm -r clean && rm -rf node_modules"`. In pnpm v11/12, `clean` is also a **built-in CLI command**, and `pnpm -r clean` can be parsed as the built-in rejecting `--recursive` rather than invoking each workspace package's `clean` script. The repository pins `pnpm@12.8.1`. All six workspace projects already have valid local `clean` scripts. Reproduced **RED** with a standalone Node source check: explicit `-r run clean` absent and implicit built-in collision present. Vendor release documentation independently confirms pnpm 11 introduced its native `clean` command.
- **Small, complete correction:** Source commit `43e552470828218917546666d55fa3ff3479f81d` changes only root cleanup invocation to `pnpm -r run clean && rm -rf node_modules`; this forces pnpm's script runner instead of its built-in command, while preserving package-by-package cleanup and root node_modules removal. Test-first commit `5a71145872defa9f7722ee4af8c98f9afa20e74b` adds `scripts/tests/workspace-clean-command.test.mjs` with 3 tests for explicit dispatch, all six local script targets/no recursive loops and no change to other workspace build/test/typecheck commands.
- **Failed/unsafe approaches avoided:** No pnpm binary install or global package-manager change, no deletion of installed production node_modules, no dependency/lockfile changes, no blanket replacement of working `pnpm -r test/typecheck`, no test-gate weakening and no accidental execution of cleanup while diagnosing. The truncated operator command could instead be an unrelated pnpm subcommand; collect complete safe argv before claiming host incident closed.
- **Verification:** GitHub fresh blobs after writes passed **8/8 source contracts**, covering exact explicit invocation, all six targets, no loops, untouched other scripts, pinned manager and three test definitions. The initial standalone Node RED was reproduced; **native committed Node test, actual pnpm v12 CLI execution, host command replay, full repository tests, CI and deployment NOT RUN**. Do not claim host command error resolved.
- **Prevention lesson:** With pnpm versions introducing built-in verbs, always use `pnpm -r run <script>` when dispatching arbitrary workspace scripts; distinguish internal script names from pnpm CLI commands. Record exact complete host command and `pnpm --version`/executable path for environment failures, never infer a source failure from a truncated shell input.
- **Next:** On current approved `Development` checkout run `node --test scripts/tests/workspace-clean-command.test.mjs` (non-destructive); validate actual desired host invocation using `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web run typecheck` or `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/worker run typecheck`. **Do not run `pnpm run clean` on production** just for verification. If the original recursive option error persists, capture the full command and sanitized `pnpm --version`, `type -a pnpm`; OPS-01 owns host toolchain. Continue FIX-01 only on a distinct, independently reproduced code defect; do not activate DEV-01 catch-up/C3/R1b or edit their files.

### FIX01-CHAT-COMPOSE-MAILBOX-DISCOVERY-20261023 — Deny unauthorized sender-address discovery
- **Role/authorization and continuity:** FIX-01 / Sentinel, direct GitHub `Development`, no feature work, no CI/Vercel polling, no edits to any Markdown except append-only root `worklog.md`. Same recovered live AGENTS/shared AGENT_MEMORY, CHAT_CONTINUITY, NEXT_ACTION, central sessions/events/issues/verification and role-local FIX-01 context as immediately preceding body-integrity correction. Source changed concurrently by DEV-01 and other FIX-01 agents; preserved those commits, especially already-implemented per-account capability diagnostics and five-day C4 UI. No real mailbox, cookie, provider or user data accessed.
- **Independent reproduced defect and scope:** `GET /api/chat/mail-compose` authenticated with the broad `organization.read` permission and immediately queried the connected primary agent mailbox addresses, returning a `mailboxes` list to the requesting user. The fixed system `viewer` role has `organization.read` but no `email.read` or `email.write`. Therefore a same-org viewer could discover connected sender addresses that the registered host `email.accounts.list` tool would not expose. `POST /api/chat/mail-compose` similarly queried account bindings before its audited tool executor checked `email.write`; although the executor prevented draft creation, a caller could probe whether a supplied account ID was bound through different API errors. This is a role/metadata leakage source defect, **not proof of cross-tenant access, a particular live request, or email delivery**.
- **RED first:** Added `apps/web/tests/chat-mail-compose-access.test.mjs` at `6b5b21c8d7ac669d83ed23f2e2e13e1000e0f0f5` with explicit owner/viewer/read-only/write-only permission cases; source boundaries assert an unauthorized GET returns 403 before mailbox query and unauthorized POST returns 403 before binding lookup, preserving audited host tool execution/no direct provider send. The original GET/POST had no email read/write gate before `primaryMailboxes`.
- **Smallest fix:** `22ad7d085e067f4fd71e71a19a4a60e2e280e0bf` adds pure `apps/web/src/lib/mail/compose-access.ts` predicates: `mayDiscoverComposeMailboxes` allows actors with `email.read` OR `email.write`, and `mayWriteComposeDraft` requires explicit `email.write`. `5d7ffd44af209862f5415daf1279d9118bfae8b0` calls the appropriate predicate immediately after trusted organization/user/agent authentication and BEFORE calling the mailbox DB query or tool registry: unauthorized GET/POST return 403 with non-identifying actionable messages. Users with read-only mail permission retain GET discovery but not draft/approval mutation, and authorized write-only actors retain the ability to select their assigned mailbox. The host executor continues enforcing agent policy, tenant/primary binding, provider capabilities, content schema, durable audit and separate human send approval.
- **GREEN evidence:** Re-fetched exact GitHub-committed route/helper/test and preceding body-preservation and per-mailbox readiness source. Executed the committed pure helper predicates in isolated V8 with only TypeScript parameter annotations removed: **5/5 permission case scenarios PASS** (viewer, read-only, write-only, read+write, none). **10/10 fresh source integration contracts PASS** (both guards precede account inspection, both 403s, untouched host executor/no send, role data, test presence, unchanged capability readiness and body integrity). No full native `node --experimental-strip-types --test`, web Vitest, web TS, authenticated browser, PostgreSQL member/tenant fixtures, provider, deployment or email delivery run; don't report runtime acceptance.
- **Failed approaches avoided:** Did not grant `email.write` to any system role, treat organization-wide membership as mail read permission, reveal account existence to unauthorized callers through binding error differences, change the saved agent policy, bypass host authorization/approval, disable unrelated chat/catch-up/voice features, log copied browser cookies or run production requests. No new migration or deployment. The original user's GET response with `canDraft:false` remains expected under roles without `email.write`.
- **Next action:** On a sanitized dependency-equipped exact-current-`Development` checkout run `node --experimental-strip-types --test apps/web/tests/chat-mail-compose-access.test.mjs apps/web/tests/chat-mail-compose-readiness.test.mjs` and `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web typecheck`; then authenticated DB/API test same-org viewer (403/zero mailbox metadata), email.read nonwriter (GET allowed, POST denied), granted writer (binding+policy+provider gating, draft-created and subsequent approval-pending only), unbound account probing negative and tenant-A/B. OPS-01 handles deployment, credential sanitation and real live verification; DEV-01/project controller owns separate least-privilege user-level `email.write` authorization feature. Continue other independent FIX-01 error repairs without waiting for Gates/CI/Vercel.

### FIX01-CHAT-VOICE-EMAIL-INTENT-FALSE-NEGATIVE-20261023 — verified email action feedback, 2026-10-23

- **Ownership, baseline and recovery:** FIX-01/Sentinel source-error remediation only on direct `Development`; initial current HEAD `6b5b21c8d7ac669d83ed23f2e2e13e1000e0f0f5`. - **Symptom and root cause (independent reproducible regression):** The shared pure `isExplicitEmailMutationRequest` classifier in `apps/web/src/lib/mail/action-feedback.ts` was used by both `/api/chat/stream` and `/api/voice/turn` to require verified host outcomes for outgoing mail commands. However, it returned **false** for ordinary valid variants including `send emails to ...` (plural), `send these messages to ...`, `please email ...`, `could you respond to this email`, `I want to send an email ...`, `I'd like to draft ...`, and `can you please send ...`. These cases could skip early `email.write` denial and the mandatory verified mutation-result reporting, letting a tool-free/provider completion appear more authoritative than its host proof. This does **not** prove that any mail was actually sent; the real tool executor continues to enforce actor privileges, account binding, capabilities, per-agent policy, audited execution and separate human send approval. Baseline locally reproduced six initial false classifications from the original regex, without any provider or database access.
- **Why tempting approaches fail:** Checking 16 UI-selected agent tools does not grant the current user `email.write`, and the role catalogue does not grant it by default. Re-enabling the old direct `/api/chat/mail-compose` interception would silently hide the AI turn; allowing the LLM to report a send without audited tool result would invent delivery. Treating every `send me a summary/list of emails` phrase as outbound would also unnecessarily block legitimate mailbox reads. No role grants, saved policy, mailbox binding, feature activation, auto-send or approval bypass were attempted.
- **Complete RED→GREEN correction:** Added explicit negative/positive regression cases to the existing native Node suite `apps/web/tests/chat-voice-email-action-feedback.test.mjs` in `e39666ef98c773a86a02ef31fed7d567704c2675`, then expanded common first-person and polite forms in `b03f1bca64a77792fd7356fd941a51641586c39b`. Fixed `isExplicitEmailMutationRequest` in `a7fb5a0357efe319e570cd59d016af31fc4ad78a` to cover plural email/messages, recipient-addressed `send` commands and `email/respond to` verbs; refined optional `I want to`, `I would like to`, `I'd like to`, and `Can you please` in `95240febd4e13f6af66424f6565d3b95673e21f7`. Keep read-only `send me the emails from yesterday`, `send me a summary of emails` and informational questions out of the mutation gate. Added tool-outcome guidance RED `41f15a7f144e8d4cc4f37f2308f1c689a79c00be`, then `a6e84e289b1d2afa35faf91994e9c41387922107` updates `verifiedEmailToolSummary`: fixed allowlisted host error codes now give permission/policy/mailbox/provider/approval-specific next actions; unknown internal errors remain redacted. Verified draft success instructs review; `pending_approval` directs to Mail > Approvals. It never claims a send was performed. Trims/lowercases addresses only; retains reviewed email body verbatim; allows empty subject/body for local drafts (human send approval separately requires complete reviewed content). `apps/web/src/app/api/mail/drafts/route.ts` (`48cf3b71b89c5f716a53a24497f02aa3a0eeede7`) now calls `validateDraftWrite(body, 'create'|'patch')` before either repository mutation; invalid payloads return `invalid_draft_payload` HTTP 422. Preserves actor `email.write`, same-origin check, tenant-scoped repositories and existing approval lock/audit requirements. Did not edit `hasDraftChanges` compatibility helper or the authorized send/approval flow.

### FIX01-EMAIL-SENT-DRAFT-RECONCILIATION-20261023 — Preserve local draft immutability after provider send

- **Authorization / branch recovery:** Owner explicitly directed FIX-01 / Sentinel to continue **independently reproducible code-error fixes** directly on `Rilan-Dev/ai-comms-agent` `Development`, never block on CI/Vercel/gates and do not edit any Markdown except append-only **root `worklog.md`**. Recovered latest starting HEAD `2ac6f61a326ae8508db14a8a67e8506c70793f53`, read-only `AGENTS.md`, `docs/project-control/CHAT_CONTINUITY.md`, `NEXT_ACTION.md`, shared `AGENT_MEMORY.md`, central `AGENT_SESSIONS.md`/`AGENT_BACKLOG.md`, FIX-01 role `MEMORY.md`/`WORKLOG.md` and current email action/draft sources. Phase 4 remains implementation in progress. No DEV-01 feature redesign, system-role `email.write` grants, production mail, external provider calls, schema/migrations, deployment/OPS-01 work, PR or new branch.
- **Confirmed root cause:** Both `apps/worker/src/email/action-worker.ts` and `action-executor.ts` correctly mark the durable `email_actions` row `completed` after provider send, **then** best-effort reconcile the local draft. Before this fix, `packages/db/src/email/repository.ts` `updateLocalDraftForOrganization`, `deleteLocalDraftForOrganization`, `queueLocalDraftProviderSyncForOrganization` and `claimQueuedProviderDraftSyncForOrganization` excluded pending (unexpired), approved (unexpired) and executing sends, but **did not exclude completed actions**. This created a window in which the draft could be edited, deleted or queued as an outgoing provider draft after delivery but before the local `is_draft=false` reconciliation, making the locally displayed content diverge from the immutable human-reviewed payload. More importantly, stale email execution recovery records `failed` with `result.providerOutcomeUnknown=true`; allowing such a draft to be rewritten or deleted could erase evidence of a potentially accepted provider message. Finally, `markLocalDraftSentForOrganization` lacked `m.is_draft=true` and could overwrite a no-longer-draft message's provider ID/state if called again. These failures were demonstrated from the specific SQL predicates and order of durable transitions, **not** observed in a production mailbox.
- **Reproduction before source change:** Added test-first `apps/worker/tests/sent-draft-lifecycle.test.mjs` commit `594e9f9f46e5a493213824139d1534b98083a814`: source-level assertions for same-tenant **still-draft-only** reconciliation, immutable completed-send parent action across four mutation paths, retained active pending/approved lock, and durable action settlement before best-effort reconciliation in both batch/one-shot executors. Initial source contracts **failed for all four completed-send guards and for the still-draft reconciliation predicate**. While the fix was in progress, a further test-first commit `da1dc45ba4b56aa3954b81c7e76e958490a9529f` specifically required unknown-provider-outcome `failed` actions to remain locked but ordinary definite failures not to be blanket-locked. This also failed against the then-current code. No dependencies or DB were invoked while defining RED.
- **Safe repair:** `147fc6321ca7da0c7f031225262eaf59a5bd9313` extended the four tenant-and-account-scoped email action checks to block `ea.status IN ('executing','completed')`; made `markLocalDraftSentForOrganization` require `m.is_draft = true`. `323d8e6a3021661de4c15c98ff7d3afc18c65758` additionally blocks `ea.status='failed'` **only** when JSON result's `providerOutcomeUnknown` is true, preserving existing ability to edit or retry definitely failed ordinary actions and expired/rejected approvals. The previous explicit pending/approved expiration guards remain unchanged. Existing test `apps/worker/tests/approved-email-snapshot-integrity.test.ts` updated in `53e1af30124a055ee51bc17b195e8d5ab8b9cc16` to assert both completed and time-bounded pending locks instead of a stale single `IN` pattern. `apps/web/src/app/api/mail/drafts/route.ts` updated in `a005025e20bd75667a4e15b8b13009db01e356fb` to explain locked completed actions truthfully with existing 409/404 semantics; no response shapes or authorization thresholds changed. Worker send, provider APIs, approvals, credentials and DB migrations remain unchanged.
- **Evidence after fix:** Fresh-fetched committed `Development` DB/test/API/worker blobs verified **11/11 focused source invariants PASS**: all four mutation paths exclude completed and unknown-outcome failed sends; preserve tenant ID + mailbox + draft match and age-scoped pending/approved locks; reconciler requires still-draft state; both existing worker paths settle durable action before local reconciliation; stale recovery sets `providerOutcomeUnknown`; new and amended regressions present; UI error refers to completed send. No current PostgreSQL checkout/daemon or equipped repo installation in this sandbox; **NOT RUN**: the committed native `node --test apps/worker/tests/sent-draft-lifecycle.test.mjs`, `node --import tsx --test apps/worker/tests/approved-email-snapshot-integrity.test.ts`, web/DB/worker typechecks, disposable Postgres concurrency tests for settlement vs PATCH/DELETE/provider sync, browser, live provider delivery, GitHub Actions, Vercel or deployed runtime. Static contract evidence is **not** runtime acceptance.
- **Failed/unsafe alternatives avoided:** No automatic replay of unknown provider outcomes, force-resetting unknown actions to approved, hiding completion errors, creating duplicate remote drafts, deleting audit/action rows, direct mail sends, broad system-role permission elevation, tenant-unscoped SQL, locking all `failed` actions forever, or treating an empty draft list as an unverified email delivery. Existing human approval requirement and worker settlement remain intact.
- **Prevention lesson:** Side-effect lifecycle immutability must cover the **post-provider-success / pre-local-reconciliation** gap and **ambiguous failure** state, not merely pending and executing statuses. Require the draft's current state in the reconciliation SQL; guard edit/delete/provider-sync claim with the same tenant+mailbox+action status rule. Do not treat provider-unknown failed actions as definite non-delivery.
- **Next and owner separation:** In a dependency-equipped sanitized checkout at exact `Development` SHA run `node --test apps/worker/tests/sent-draft-lifecycle.test.mjs`, `cd apps/worker && node --import tsx --test tests/approved-email-snapshot-integrity.test.ts tests/email-action-security.test.ts`, and root `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/db typecheck && pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/worker typecheck && pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web typecheck`. In disposable org A/B PostgreSQL test completed-send update/delete/sync rejection, provider-unknown failed rejection, ordinary failed and rejected/expired amendment allowed, and repeated reconcile not modifying a sent row. OPS-01 owns infrastructure/deploy/provider/credential/live-email acceptance; DEV-01 owns user-level email.write RBAC design and product onboarding. Continue to the next **distinct, reproduced** FIX-01 defect; do not poll CI or Vercel. No real email was drafted, sent, approved or changed in this session.

## FIX01-MANUAL-COMPOSE-ASYNC-SCOPE-20261023 — FIX-01 / Sentinel — Development

- **Scope and recovery:** Same user-authorized direct-Development error-only FIX-01 role, while DEV-01 continues feature development; recovered current GitHub HEAD and read `docs/project-control/CHAT_CONTINUITY.md`, `NEXT_ACTION.md` and FIX memory read-only. - **Independent source defect (RED):** `apps/web/src/app/mail/compose/compose-client.tsx` previously loaded an existing draft and mailbox list using uncancelled asynchronous `fetch` promise chains. Navigating `draftId=A → B` or between organizations while GET A was slow let the late response reattach A's `draftId`, recipients, sending mailbox, subject/body and already-reviewed approval snapshot under the B UI. The account discovery callback likewise could attach a former organization's default sending mailbox. Earlier concurrent POST save or approval responses could mutate local review or approval state after navigation. This is a real stale-context security/UI source defect; no claim actual external send occurred.
- **Regression first:** Added `apps/web/tests/manual-compose-async-scope-race.test.ts` in commit `966aaa8df364acda9c034125c5168bb67d943156`. It asserts AbortController cleanup, response identity checks, cleared reviewed/draft/action state on navigation, independent account-list cancellation, scoped sender default selection, disabled mutation during transition, and revision fencing of save/approval completion. The pre-fix client lacks all guards.
- **Complete correction:** `apps/web/src/app/mail/compose/compose-client.tsx` commits `f065c71e56663a5709a57e75ab77bac3eed45b36`, `afae3ec633a990bd255ecbc6f326ec02cbdb1338`, `a14cfd3e3e8a2a8e351658a72c73505fac77f2bd`. A stable org/draft scope plus strictly increasing revision prevents A→B→A ABA stale completions. Changing selection resets stale `draftId`, `approvalId`, `reviewedSnapshot`, timestamps, mailbox fields and editable content; loading and mailbox fetches are aborted on effect cleanup, and fulfilled requests recheck the current revision/org before changing UI. The previous tenant's connected mailbox cannot become a new tenant's default. Save and approval POST completions are rejected if a new revision is active; per-revision in-flight locks prevent a late old operation releasing a new operation. UI review/save controls stay disabled while the new scope is not yet initialized. Existing human approval and immutable reviewed snapshot validation remain unchanged. No backend permission or human-send gate was bypassed.
- **Preexisting test alignment:** Updated `apps/web/tests/manual-compose-review-integrity.test.ts` expectations in `8da040e52b27ac82aaae2138a2ee4e3873a0eb6c` and `8935df70d4aad97b75939639e988dad440b20775`, matching the stronger revision-scoped approval lock and sender dropdown guard; updated dedicated race test in `10cfc8bba46547bc77dc31abb3f663efd56a4aa9`, `e3eba61e7f5f3cda203b3d3170915d6f43a6dbae` for session revision and tenant-account fencing. Updated `apps/web/tests/mail-draft-noop-privacy.test.mjs` `7b0dc778b465d630167922c773879e2270f8cb39` to require the new strict create/edit validator from the immediately preceding work slice, without weakening the standalone hasDraftChanges privacy helper.
- **Executed focused GREEN:** Directly executed the committed `saveDraft()` function with mocked deferred HTTP replies and shared React/ref state: **4/4 PASS** (late draft A does not reattach under B, A's finally cannot release B's in-flight lock, B response attaches only B's own record, B releases its lock). Ran final committed async-scope Vitest callback assertions **3/3 PASS** and original manual-review integrity callback assertions including actual pure review helper **5/5 PASS**, total **12/12 targeted checks PASS**. Also executed existing no-op draft privacy Node-style contract callback cases **4/4 PASS**, preserving the no-zero-field/malformed-PATCH early denial after the preceding validator change. Source code/exact GitHub content exercised with minimal test/assertion shims; **NOT** native pnpm Vitest, full TypeScript compiler, React browser, Postgres/provider or deployed end-to-end acceptance. No external email sent or permission grants changed.
