### FIX01-EMAIL-READ-FOLDER-LABEL-SQL-PARAMS-20261023 — FIX-01 / Sentinel, Development source correction

- **Owner authorization/state:** Owner explicitly requested FIX-01 implementation error repair only, direct `Development` commits, regression evidence, root-only append `worklog.md`, no other Markdown changes, no waiting for GitHub Actions/Vercel/OPS-01/DEV-01. Recovered current `Development` starting at `02f24576ced2180bac7e9f1ca540bb2b25ba6b82`, read `AGENTS.md`, `docs/project-control/CHAT_CONTINUITY.md`, `NEXT_ACTION.md`, FIX-01 role-local memory/worklog, issues and verification state. Phase 4 stays implementation-in-progress; recent DEV-01 added owner/admin `email.write` with human approval still separately required. That role-grant issue was already done and was not touched here.
- **Independent reproducible defect:** `packages/email-agent-runtime/src/email-read-tools.ts` `listMessages` uses an `add((n)=>SQL,value)` builder which appends the filter value as a PostgreSQL bind parameter. But its optional folder predicate interpolated `${n}` directly into SQL four times (instead of PostgreSQL `$${n}`), yielding e.g. `m.folder_id::text = 3` and `f.name ILIKE 3` for an ordinary filter at index 3; this can fail PostgreSQL text-vs-integer comparison rather than search the chosen folder. The optional label predicate also used `jsonb_build_array(${n}::text)` rather than the actual label parameter, silently searching for label text `3` instead of the supplied label. Other filters correctly used `$${n}`. Neither issue required provider access, a permission change or a schema migration.
- **Regression before correction / RED:** Created dependency-free native Node test `apps/worker/tests/email-read-filter-sql.test.mjs`, commit `b33f3a352ee1b1e103398f7e4f21d04322cd0a8d`. It reads the ACTUAL repository filter template source and runs each compiled fragment in a sandboxed VM for indexes 3 and 11, requires four bound occurrences in folder comparisons, correct bound label JSONB containment, and preserved organization/account scoping. Executing the same committed template expressions against the initial GitHub source produced **4/8 focused checks PASS**: folder generated `= 3`/`ILIKE 3`, label generated `jsonb_build_array(3::text)`, while existing tenant/account invariants passed. The test was added before the corrected source.
- **Failed approach caught and corrected:** Two update attempts using JavaScript `String.replace(old, replacementString)` inadvertently normalized the literal `$$` characters in the replacement string into `$` (JavaScript replacement-string expansion), resulting in unchanged GitHub file blobs despite commits `51c08544` and its earlier update attempt; concurrent different-agent commits were also present. Explicit readback of file SHA `a42c0cc3` and generated SQL detected the no-op. The final rewrite used `String.replace(old, () => replacementString)`, which preserves the literal `$${n}` template expressions, and checked changed content BEFORE commit. Do not confuse a commit message with proof that the intended blob changed.
- **Remedy / GREEN:** Commit `d0db1af6bd7b68b8df56e8d1cc4f0aaa97ae42aa` changes only the two affected optional SQL fragment builders (four folder operands and one label operand), preserving existing parameter bindings, tenant isolation, account binding, provider behavior, authorization and read-only semantics. GitHub re-fetch proved the source blob changed from `a42c0cc3` to `f35f0004`. Executing actual committed fragments for n=3 and n=11 now produces `m.folder_id::text = $3`, the corresponding scoped folder predicates at `$3`, and `m.labels @> jsonb_build_array($3::text)` (and likewise `$11`); **10/10 focused executed source checks PASS**, including no integer literals and unchanged tenant/account guards.
- **Evidence boundary / unresolved:** The native Node `node --test apps/worker/tests/email-read-filter-sql.test.mjs` test file is committed, but has **not** run in a complete dependency-equipped repository checkout. - **Independent reproducible implementation defect (RED):** Three separate WhatsApp approval creation boundaries allowed a repeated org/idempotency key to resolve an arbitrary pre-existing approval if the *payload* appeared identical, without proving the original requester was the same and without requiring the approval to remain pending and unexpired. Agent `apps/worker/src/agent/whatsapp-tool-runtime.ts::createWhatsAppSendApprovalTool`, manual `apps/web/src/app/api/whatsapp/messages/route.ts::POST`, and scheduled product `apps/worker/src/agent/product-action-executor.ts` used INSERT ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING followed by a SELECT of old row/status; agent and product recorded a fresh 'pending' audit even for approved/rejected/queued/sent/expired action. Manual route returned its action ID and status to a distinct requester who reused the key. This is a source-level actor/approval identity problem; no evidence of an actual customer incident or WhatsApp message sent.
- **Regression first:** Commit 9bc87f803d92fdf554e6c7a89ad4398a96b01e29 added `apps/worker/tests/whatsapp-approval-retry-immutability.test.ts` requiring all three surfaces to atomically bind the exact requester, agent, account, recipient, message body, type, template name/language/jsonb parameters, pending status and expiry. Existing source failed because no conditional conflict UPDATE existed. Corrected test-only whitespace expectation d418a1c790cb56d2258991462ccd6b6c6ab7aabf. Extended existing worker Vitest send-tool suite in 369ec11a2603f171f63dc9009cc32a8992a8e447 to cover a valid identical pending replay and an empty result representing a decided/expired/different-actor collision (no false pending audit). Existing normal insert/mock contract remains compatible.
- **Complete correction at all creation boundaries:** Agent commit 9cd611d30fef8d21ca6764141b90159cdccdad8b, manual API commit b945bdc5894d7f31a08f4a114c775babccbc5a59, and product executor commit ad66489ab71e0083452e303fea8ff023a75f1640 replace DO NOTHING+SELECT with a single conditional `ON CONFLICT (organization_id,idempotency_key) WHERE idempotency_key IS NOT NULL DO UPDATE SET idempotency_key=EXCLUDED.idempotency_key WHERE ... RETURNING id,status`. The WHERE requires the original row's `status='pending'`, `expires_at>now()`, same `requested_by` (IS NOT DISTINCT), `agent_id`, account, recipient, message, payload type, template name/language/parameters; the agent/product also require same binding-required flag. If collision predicate fails PostgreSQL returns no row, not a foreign/terminal action ID. Agent returns `idempotency_conflict` without an audit; manual API returns HTTP409 `idempotency_conflict`; product executor returns failed action with no false 'pending_approval' result or audit. Existing human approval, provider capability, Meta 24h/template validation and worker queuing boundaries unchanged; no automatic send.

First exploratory product mock omitted `type`, causing a test-harness-only ReferenceError; added required product scope and reran all 6/6 PASS without code changes. Generated SQL now includes `f.id = m.folder_id`, `f.email_account_id = m.email_account_id`, `f.organization_id = $1`, and four `$3` values. This tests the compiled SQL expression shape only.

### FIX01-SEND-DRAFT-APPROVAL-IDEMPOTENCY-20261023 — FIX-01 / Sentinel (2026-10-23)

- **Owner scope/recovery:** Owner repeatedly directed FIX-01 source-only error fixes directly on `Rilan-Dev/ai-comms-agent/tree/Development`, no CI/Vercel/gates waiting, no DEV-01 product-feature takeover, and **no Markdown edits except an append to lowercase root `worklog.md`**. Recovered current `Development` starting HEAD `b174b7d1c61fabbdb10de12f67b2244197c7c23b`, `AGENTS.md`, shared `AGENT_MEMORY.md`, central `AGENT_EVENTS/SESSIONS/HANDOFF`, FIX-01 role-local `MEMORY/WORKLOG`, `CHAT_CONTINUITY.md`, `NEXT_ACTION.md`, `PROJECT_STATE.md`, `CURRENT_PHASE.md`, and `VERIFICATION_STATE.md`. Phase 4 still `implementation_in_progress`; C3 worker and standing routine/WhatsApp send remain gated. DEV-01 concurrent catch-up/preview features and owner/admin `email.write` grant had already been implemented: do **not** duplicate them. OPS-01 handles deployment/credentials/CI/production.
- **Independent reproducible defect:** Manual Compose `apps/web/src/app/mail/compose/compose-client.tsx` requests `POST /api/mail/actions` for `send_email` without `Idempotency-Key` or body `idempotencyKey`. API treated absent key as `null`; shared `packages/db/src/email/actions.ts` `createEmailAction` `ON CONFLICT (organization_id,idempotency_key) WHERE idempotency_key IS NOT NULL` consequently never deduplicated those attempts. Two otherwise identical HTTP requests for the same draft created two distinct `pending_approval` rows, which authorized humans could independently approve, risking duplicate provider sends. Chat/voice host-tool requests used separate per-turn keys, so their differing keys also failed to protect a shared draft across surfaces. The incident is source-reproduced; **no duplicate email was actually observed or sent in this turn**.
- **Why a UI-only debounce is insufficient:** Concurrent retries, browser restarts, lost responses, different clients, and separate human-agent tool calls cannot be reliably deduplicated by React state or arbitrary request-provided keys. Relying on `ON CONFLICT` when keys are nullable or differ by caller is insufficient. Dropping existing approval or reactivating terminal rows is also unsafe.
- **Regression RED first:** Added `apps/web/tests/email-send-draft-idempotency.test.mjs` in direct-Development commit `3e709701f43dd1bb637e52175893fa9e0e3548fc` asserting durable draft row locking, same-draft existing-action lookup **before** insert, reusable identical still-pending review only for matching actor/agent/content, rejected settled/conflicting approvals, existing manual API 409 and agent's typed conflict, preservation of reply/forward contracts and no provider send in API route. Evaluated exact **pre-fix** GitHub `createEmailAction` function from `b174b7d` in isolated JS with fake SQL (only TypeScript type annotation/generics removed): **0/8 protection scenarios passed**, eight action rows inserted across attempted repeats, actor/content/agent changes and settled/expired retries. No actual provider or DB used.
- **Smallest shared correction:** `packages/db/src/email/actions.ts` commit `f98d36143f15ec0bb94a5913a84f31df3464010d` routes **saved-draft `send_email`** approvals through a single `withTransaction`, first locking the verified `email_messages` draft by ID, organization, account and `is_draft=true` using `FOR UPDATE`. While holding the row lock it checks any previous `send_email` action for that draft regardless of idempotency key. A matching **still-pending** action with exact content hash, requester, and agent returns the same durable action ID. Different user/agent/content or settled/expired action raises the existing typed `EmailActionIdempotencyConflictError`, rather than creating a second action. Only a draft with no prior send action inserts a new pending approval, still using the existing conditional unique key fallback. Source-only follow-ups: `12b598e964bf6d27ad4422187e9b4dc6e635c469` uses PostgreSQL `now()` for pending-expiry classification, not web-server clock; `99396242b62532e0c9afa2cf0696f1d272129daf` makes the 409 conflict text accurately describe already-reviewed/settled drafts. Regression updates `7b6464d87b1cc1ec1d8ef725f6be941ccfe74917` and `5787a6ad2a028ca83325feb94d472c9a11a86b4d` keep existing typed agent-tool conflict and DB expiry contracts.
- **Scope preserved:** The legacy source-snapshot-only `send_email` path without a saved draft (cross-surface harness), reply and forward action contracts continue unchanged. No database migrations, auth/role grants, agent policy changes, new sending capability, autoapproval, direct provider operation, new scheduled worker, external email, or user-visible feature added. `apps/web/src/app/api/mail/actions/route.ts` already catches `EmailActionIdempotencyConflictError` as 409; `packages/email-agent-runtime/src/email-write-tools.ts` already exposes `email_action_conflict` safely to agent callers, so do not duplicate those code changes. A settled draft requires a **new saved draft** for another send, rather than silently resurrecting approval.
- **GREEN evidence:** Re-fetched exact corrected GitHub function and compiled it with *only* TS annotation/generics removed into a V8 sandbox with mocked `withTransaction` and `query` (no real DB). **8/8** behavioral scenarios passed: initial creates one row, same draft retry with a different key returns identical action ID, other user denied, changed content denied, other agent denied, completed denied, expired denied, and another draft allowed. Exact pre-fix source under the same harness **0/8**, creating eight rows; post-fix created two distinct draft rows only. Executed the **four committed source-contract test callbacks** using GitHub-fetched source and a mock `readFileSync`/Node-test/assert API: **4/4 PASS**. PostgreSQL locking syntax, transaction and authorization mechanisms were inspected in source, not executed on a database.
- **Verification limits:** The isolated mock **cannot prove** PostgreSQL isolation, interleaving of concurrent clients, row-lock behavior or provider delivery; native committed Node test (via `node --test`), installed web/DB test suites, TypeScript typechecks, disposable PostgreSQL transaction test, authenticated browser, CI/Vercel, deployment and production were **NOT RUN**. The branch is source-corrected, not production-approved.
- **Prevention:** Idempotency for an irreversible side effect must be anchored in a durable entity (a saved draft), not a missing/optional or per-channel request key. Serialize competing create attempts on that entity inside the same database transaction and return only a safe existing pending review for identical identity and content; never turn a stale or settled approval back into `pending_approval`.
- **Next FIX-01 / OPS-01 action:** On an equipped sanitized `Development` checkout run `node --test apps/web/tests/email-send-draft-idempotency.test.mjs`, existing `apps/web/tests/email-action-contract.test.ts`, DB/email-agent-runtime typechecks, and disposable PostgreSQL two-client simultaneous `createEmailAction` calls for one draft (null vs different keys, different users/agents/content, expired/completed, and different tenant/account). ### FIX01-SEND-DRAFT-REVIEW-LOCK-20261023 — Follow-on to same FIX-01 saved-draft approval lifecycle (2026-10-23)

- **Distinct RED during completion of the touched work:** The earlier same-draft duplicate-action fix correctly acquired `email_messages ... FOR UPDATE`, but the manual `/api/mail/actions` route and agent tool read their recipient/subject/body snapshot **before** entering `createEmailAction`. A competing authorized draft PATCH could commit between that read and the transaction lock. Merely locking before inserting then still accepted the now-stale reviewed content, enabling approval for an out-of-date snapshot while the physical draft differed. The in-repository `matchesManualComposeReview` protects an earlier browser read, not a concurrent edit after that server read. This was independently reproduced in an exact-source isolated mocked SQL harness: at commit `99396242b62532e0c9afa2cf0696f1d272129daf` with draft persisted as `Changed after review` versus supplied `Original reviewed`, the action was created (RED: 1 row). This is a source-test reproduction, **not a witnessed production race**.
- **Test before remedy:** Expanded the existing native source contract `apps/web/tests/email-send-draft-idempotency.test.mjs` in commit `2a71b4e46fe030e77772b7f2b9db0adaccb4025f` to require the locked `SELECT` to return current To/CC/BCC, subject and body, then to recompare the reviewed snapshot before any insert. Updated assertion `34f9310eb39fd6f3175342f4624b72ade53298c8` after the lock query was expanded; test remains anchored to exact production SQL/JS ordering rather than the old column list.
- **Small focused remedy:** `dc240005020aa2bc489d298e0dfa6148e36a7570` in `packages/db/src/email/actions.ts` adds a pure internal `matchesPersistedSendDraftSnapshot` check **after acquiring the existing same-org/same-account/same-draft `FOR UPDATE` row lock and before prior-action lookup/insert**. It compares all three ordered recipient arrays case-insensitively with trimmed email addresses, plus exact persisted subject and complete body including whitespace; missing or malformed recipient arrays fail closed, optional empty CC/BCC are supported. A mismatch raises existing typed approval-conflict, never creates a new approval or calls a provider. This reuses the same DB transaction and preserves the non-draft legacy action contract and explicit human approval. No schema, permission, policy, direct send or migration change.

### FIX01-EMAIL-ACTION-ROW-NARROWING-20261023 — FIX-01 / Sentinel — Strict TypeScript worker build regression
- **Request and recovery:** User supplied fresh local Development console output: `git pull --ff-only origin Development` fast-forwarded local checkout through `42d7b27a`, then `bash scripts/update-and-up.sh` failed at Docker worker build (`pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/worker build`; exit 2). The exact six compiler diagnostics were `TS2345` and `TS18048` in `packages/db/src/email/actions.ts` at lines 138 and 150–154. Recovered current GitHub Development HEAD `db6c61ab1d6be63633c184e80b1c8bb26316d775`, root `AGENTS.md`, `docs/project-control/CHAT_CONTINUITY.md`, `docs/project-control/NEXT_ACTION.md`, current DB email actions and related regression tests before changes. Work is FIX-01 source-only directly on Development; DEV-01 feature changes preserved, OPS-01 owns Docker/build/deployment infrastructure and environment.
- **Root cause:** Recent same-draft send-approval idempotency logic checks `locked.rows.length` then passes `locked.rows[0]` to a strict `Row` parameter, and checks `prior.rows.length` then dereferences `prior.rows[0]`. With strict `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`, the index still has type `Row | undefined`; TypeScript does not narrow an array index from a length or repeated index check. The insertion and fallback paths similarly read their first row repeatedly instead of narrowing one captured value. This breaks worker TypeScript compilation even though the optimistic DB behavior is runtime-safe in normal cases. The supplied log independently demonstrates the exact failing source file; this session did **not** reproduce the complete worker build in an equipped checkout.
- **RED regression first:** `apps/web/tests/email-send-draft-idempotency.test.mjs` commit `4d29b988f8493a2657265959459e74807e907d0e` adds named row-guard assertions for locked saved draft, existing pending action, insert and fallback insert, while updating earlier snapshot-guard expectations to the bound `lockedDraft` value. Fetched prior GitHub source blob `dc5ff49c771e92a07fbbf1e4676fc2a005b223a8`: all four named captures are missing (structural RED). The original user Docker diagnostic is the broader native RED worker build evidence.
- **Correction:** `8f476cb91b6301bb2fe8bc6b09a6b640339051ce` changes **only** `packages/db/src/email/actions.ts`: `const lockedDraft = locked.rows[0]; if (!lockedDraft) throw...` before snapshot validation; `const existing = prior.rows[0]; if (existing) {...}` before pending/hash/actor/agent checks; `const insertedRow = inserted.rows[0]` and `const resultRow = r.rows[0]` before checking/returning. No casts or `!` assertions suppress type safety. The original same-org/email-account draft lock, reviewed recipient/body snapshot comparison, one-pending-review per draft, idempotency conflict logic, and human-send-approval lifecycle remain unchanged.
- **Verification:** Fetched the committed GitHub Development source and committed tests; **13/13** focused structural/regression compatibility checks pass across every source guard and the idempotency/transactional contracts. Ran independent system `tsc --strict --noUncheckedIndexedAccess --noEmit --skipLibCheck --target ES2022` on standalone reproductions of the exact unsafe array-row pattern and its guarded replacement: old code **exit 2** (TS2345, TS18048), guarded code **exit 0**. This isolated strict-TS reproduction is **not** a full native `@ai-comms-agent/worker` build or repo typecheck. Exact Node regression, full database/worker/web tests, authenticated DB concurrency, Docker image build, GitHub Actions, Vercel and production acceptance **not executed** in this GitHub-only environment. Do not claim deployment fixed until equipped host run.
- **Failed/unsafe alternatives avoided:** No `any` cast, non-null assertion `!`, suppression of strict compiler options, removal of durable duplicate-approval protection, change to send permissions or approval handling, migration, provider call, or modification to other Markdown files. Do not infer Docker worker success from source checks.
- **Next action:** In the user's sanitized updated Development checkout run `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/worker build`, `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/db typecheck`, and `node --test apps/web/tests/email-send-draft-idempotency.test.mjs`; then `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web build` if appropriate under OPS-01 deployment controls. Handle any next *independent reproducible* source errors as FIX-01, without waiting for CI/Vercel. The user's `scripts/update-and-up.sh` printed `Pulling origin/main` despite the interactive prompt on Development; inspect script target/checkouts separately under OPS-01 before deployment, and don't rewrite deployment logic in this source-only task. Preserve concurrent DEV-01 changes and append remediation only to lowercase root `worklog.md`.

### FIX01-CHAT-VOICE-EMPTY-AI-STREAM-20261023 — false completed blank assistant turns

- **Owner/authorization:** Independently reproducible FIX-01 source defect on current direct `Development`, after completing `FIX01-CHAT-TEST-EMAIL-STREAM-REGRESSION-20261023`; DEV-01 product changes and OPS-01 CI/deployment remain separate. - **Symptom and root cause:** Chat model streaming branch in `apps/web/src/app/api/chat/stream/route.ts` returned `done` and persisted an assistant message after `for await (const chunk of turn.stream)` even if the provider yielded no chunks (or only whitespace). Voice model streaming branch in `apps/web/src/app/api/voice/turn/route.ts` likewise emitted a completed turn with no `delta`, and `saveAnswer("")` silently wrote nothing. This can produce a completed-but-empty interaction with no actionable user feedback. Unlike prior test-email *client* interception, the failure is after request dispatch when the upstream model emits nothing. Source inspection reproduces the control flow; no claim about a particular live provider response.
- **Complete regression-first remedy:** `c1b268d60bc87f5a2c5597702f4742721bf13bac` adds a three-case `apps/web/tests/chat-voice-empty-stream.test.mjs` covering Chat, Voice and unchanged host-trusted email read/tool feedback contracts. `5a1b6f3d5e378c4ccafc89f3e8df3523a20fb26a` checks `assistantOutput.trim()` before assistant persistence and `done` in the Chat SSE path, throwing to the existing sanitized SSE `error` on an empty response. `cc7109509bd1d668aa2724aa765ab0cfe9040645` checks `deliveredText.trim()` before `saveAnswer` and `done` in the plain-model Voice SSE path, also triggering the existing sanitized error event. The separate read-only final answer path already rejects empty provider output and the host-authorized tool path supplies verified denial, draft and approval statuses. No changes to external actions, model permissions, provider secrets, migrations, or approval workflow.
- **Avoided failed/unsafe approach:** Do not mask missing provider tokens with a fabricated assistant claim (especially that mail was sent). Do not signal `done` or store a blank reply when no verified answer exists. Existing user-visible sanitized SSE `error` is the correct failure path; provider configuration/operational diagnosis remains OPS-01 after a reproduced deployed revision.
- **Evidence:** Exact GitHub re-fetch shows **8/8** targeted source assertions PASS for both empty guards before persistence/completion, preserved host verified feedback and three regression cases. The committed native Node test, package typechecks/build, real streaming provider behavior, authenticated browser and CI/Vercel/production were **NOT RUN** in this connector-only session. Source contracts are not runtime acceptance.

### FIX01-VOICE-BROWSER-ORIGIN-20261023 — complete Voice session, negotiation and speech CSRF boundary

- **Owner scope / project recovery:** Acting as FIX-01 / Sentinel on direct `Development` under latest owner instruction to correct independently reproducible source defects, preserve DEV-01 separate product changes, and not touch Markdown except this append-only root `worklog.md`. Recovered GitHub current branch `bf7f390f6009ec16939444a0fd7e8fa3e529d000`, `CHAT_CONTINUITY.md`, `NEXT_ACTION.md`, shared `AGENT_MEMORY.md`, central agent records, current phase/issues/verification, FIX-01 role-local memory/worklog and current chat/voice/tool authorization. Phase 4 remains in progress; source work is not a deployment approval. No GitHub Actions/Vercel polling.
- **Symptom / reproduced root cause:** Chat already enforces `assertSameOrigin(request)` and `/api/voice/turn` was already guarded on the *latest source*, but seven other browser-controlled Voice HTTP handlers never invoked the same exact-Origin check: `/api/voice/session` POST/PATCH/DELETE; `/api/voice/realtime` POST (provider SDP); `/api/voice/gemini/session` POST (short-lived token); `/api/voice/speech/transcribe` POST; `/api/voice/speech/speak` POST. The shared browser `startVoiceRelaySession` for two provider routes, `/api/voice/sarvam/session` and `/api/voice/elevenlabs/session`, also lacked it. All endpoints authenticate and check scoped session ownership, which must remain, but they should reject explicit browser requests from another site **before** parsing body, creating sessions, mutating lifecycle, minting tokens or invoking paid provider APIs. This finding is based on exact fetched source absence, not claimed exploitation of a production session.
- **Failed/non-applicable approach:** Initial diagnosis suspected `/api/voice/turn`; a fresh GitHub source check proved its existing `assertSameOrigin` guard, so **no duplicate edit to that route was made**. A blanket rule for every voice URL would also have broken the genuine server-to-server `/api/voice/{sarvam,elevenlabs}/realtime-init` bootstrap, which authenticates via `verifyVoiceRelayRequest` and returns relay credentials. Those signed relay endpoints **must not** be treated like browser requests. No host/app provider secret, relay token or user session cookie was used.
- **Test first:** `apps/web/tests/voice-request-origin-boundary.test.mjs` commit `5a3a6825ff24a14ae02ba7239ca430771df26619` contains six native Node cases: explicit foreign Origin denial (including unrelated Vercel, scheme mismatch and serialized `null`), accepted exact origin/absent Origin compatibility, all three session lifecycle handlers, OpenAI/Gemini/speech and existing turn guard, shared two-provider relay token path, signed relay bootstrap isolation, and existing session/user/agent ownership + human approvals.
- **Corrective source:** Added `assertSameOrigin(request)` and explicit `403 invalid_origin` before every browser-side DB or provider side effect in `apps/web/src/app/api/voice/session/route.ts` `e9158122334e45c0c6074d26a806673d8b249b69`, `voice/realtime` `8c6b44290354dd5b14f2335190b59dd0d4e379b0`, `voice/gemini/session` `50e47f62d052a53b231a57ea5edea9b74c03c631`, `voice/speech/transcribe` `dce86b92e0ac18aa33815e50ed44615dd5abf2b7`, and `voice/speech/speak` `27f0cbf20c38f0dd76f841b64a825251af4ad539`. Shared `apps/web/src/lib/voice/provider-relay-session.ts` `d1d226546c7d3034aac4bd208d74189d4e86061c` now protects both browser relay-token entrypoints. None of the signed `provider-relay-init.ts` or signed provider callback routes, authentication/grants, user configuration, model selection, API keys, provider sends or mail approval workflow changed.
- **Verification/evidence:** Fresh current GitHub `Development` fetched actual blobs for all changed files and regression: **7/7** direct session/realtime/Gemini/speech mutation guards PASS structural checks, and **4/4** shared-relay/signed-relay/unmodified-turn/new-regression checks PASS. The test file contains 6 cases. Native `node --test`, web Vitest/typecheck/build, actual authenticated browser/production traffic, relay credential tests, CI/Vercel and database/provider integration **NOT RUN** in connector-only environment. Source inspection is not proof of deployment acceptance. The existing origin policy intentionally accepts an absent Origin for nonbrowser API clients; this change denies **mismatched explicit** Origin and does not replace cookie SameSite/session security.
- **Prevention:** Review *every entrypoint* in a Voice action lifecycle: turn requests alone are insufficient if session creation, session state changes, provider negotiation and paid STT/TTS endpoints omit the browser origin boundary. Differentiate browser credentialed requests from cryptographically signed server-to-server relay callbacks. Keep host session/agent/organization binding and approval checks independent of origin.
- **Next exact FIX-01 acceptance on equipped checkout:** `node --experimental-strip-types --test apps/web/tests/voice-request-origin-boundary.test.mjs apps/web/tests/request-origin-write-boundary.test.mjs`; `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web exec vitest run tests/voice-realtime-transport.test.ts tests/voice-provider-relay.test.ts tests/voice-turn-lifecycle-guard.test.ts tests/voice-email-tool-runtime.test.ts`; `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web typecheck`. Authenticated disposable browser: original authorized same-origin Voice session→connect→turn→speech→end remains operational, explicit foreign Origin returns 403 before DB/provider/telemetry, and trusted relay bootstrap still accepts only valid signature + scoped token. Record current source SHA, sanitized HTTP status and provider-safe evidence. OPS-01 owns actual deployment/infrastructure, promotion and release acceptance. FIX-01 may continue independently reproducible source defects without waiting for CI/Vercel.

### FIX01-CHAT-USER-FACING-ANSWER-AND-MAIL-HANDOFF-20261023 — live Chat repetition, internal planning, and reviewed email handoff

- **User report/live read-only browser evidence:** Selected authenticated Chat conversation showed repeated identical unverified email-send sentences for several explicit send/draft requests, an earlier saved assistant answer containing provider-style internal planning ("No new emails were provided ... I should ..."), and interactive mail actions present but no confirmed email delivery. A different authenticated Overview browser tab showed zero pending approvals at its read-time; previous audit activity included email.draft.create allow, which is NOT proof of current send permission, complete approval or delivery. Browser connector here is read-only; no actual button click, provider draft/send or session membership inspection was done.
- **Root cause:** Chat's apps/web/src/app/api/chat/stream/route.ts uses a deterministic host fallback unverifiedEmailActionExplanation() for explicit email mutations without verified tool results; deniedEmailWriteExplanation() similarly returns a host-owned permission-denial string. The former was repeatedly displayed as though it were an ordinary generated model answer. Tool-free model output and final read-only provider tokens were emitted before any full-response check for recognizable private model planning, and old persisted assistant planning could be reintroduced into later model history and ambient search terms. Chat's inline Write email draft control opened the same secure compose form but did not prefill an unambiguously user-specified To address, requiring needless re-entry. No evidence justifies changing email.write grants, agent allowlist, or approval authorization automatically.
- **Test-first:** cc51d6df29f64be2866bcbe9758136115837463e adds chat-user-facing-answer-regression.test.mjs for provider planning/think tag suppression, normal answers, oversized/empty output, model boundary and meaningful security feedback. 0ef0991565b4e55354735815b0a974da0d6bd0b4 adds persisted-history poisoning guard. 7a0b28caf2bb8fd2f6685307dd49674d07a4572d adds chat-inline-mail-recipient-handoff.test.mjs for one explicitly addressed recipient, non-action/ambiguous/invalid negatives, and no browser-side send.
- **Fix:** 976706cee7abc8a44b0061c9f872c96958764608 adds pure user-facing-answer.ts; collects provider-generated text until validated before emitting/persisting instead of sending unexamined chunks (deliberate safety tradeoff: browser sees verified complete final answer rather than early token-by-token deltas). Recognizable internal planning, <think> segments, blank and over-limit output fail closed with safe SSE error, not stored as assistant reply. Deterministic send-denial and tool-unverified feedback now explicitly label themselves security checks, explain the actor-versus-agent permission distinction and direct users to reviewed Compose, Save draft and Request approval. 983c3eeb43bd5263c5f85a3ef81e50e7b5d149af excludes previously persisted unsafe assistant content from subsequent prompt and ambient mail search, without erasing old transcript. 270d17b2b9c45da4e61f2f70aa3c667c5d224748 adds exact recipient prefill when the user clicks the existing inline Write email draft button, preserving already edited To and any saved draft/approval. 5591e674df5d842e38271c186409406707df77b7 keeps the pure helper standalone for native strip-types regression tests. All original permission checks and human approval boundaries remain unchanged. User's natural language still reaches AI Chat and persisted tool feedback; clicking inline Compose only starts a review GET, never a write.
- **Executed verification:** Re-fetched exact committed pure helpers and matched local git hash-object: user-facing-answer.ts blob 5de036929a02a700cba6f6910d38032f1ae90451 and test-email-compose-intent.ts blob f9f5464c5400b643a9213d0f496ae86256529986. On Node 22 --experimental-strip-types, five isolated behavioral test groups PASS (three planning/output and two recipient/negative cases). Strict isolated TypeScript tsc --noEmit --strict --target ES2022 --module ESNext PASS for both exact helpers. Current GitHub source checks confirmed the model buffering, tool final guard, exclusion of historic planning, clear security messaging and recipient-only prefill. No provider messages or external account mutations were executed.
- **Not run / deployment limitation:** Complete checked-in Node test suites, full web+Clara TypeScript/Vitest, authenticated network/DOM interaction, tenant/role negative verification, provider/worker queued send, CI/Vercel, and production rollout are NOT VERIFIED. Provider final-answer buffering sacrifices incremental Chat text streaming until user-facing validation; follow-up UX can introduce a safely gated buffered/chunked renderer without exposing hidden planning. Heuristic planning detection is defense in depth, not absolute detection of every possible model planning string. The old private planning remains visible in historical transcript unless intentionally redacted under a separate reviewed workflow.
- **Next verification:** On Development run: node --experimental-strip-types --test apps/web/tests/chat-user-facing-answer-regression.test.mjs apps/web/tests/chat-inline-mail-recipient-handoff.test.mjs apps/web/tests/chat-voice-email-action-feedback.test.mjs apps/web/tests/test-mail-compose-intent.test.mjs apps/web/tests/chat-interactive-mail-workflow.test.mjs; pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web typecheck; pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web exec vitest run tests/chat-host-tool-loop.test.ts tests/chat-stream-error-boundary.test.ts tests/verified-tool-streaming-contract.test.ts. In a fully authorized disposable browser, confirm actor email.write vs agent draft/send-request, choose a connected primary sender, review exact To/subject/body, save draft, explicitly request approval and authorize reviewer, then verify provider Sent and received message once. Do not claim delivery from UI pending/approved status. OPS-01 owns production deployment; no CI polling or bypassed gates.

### FIX01-READONLY-TOOL-FINAL-DELIVERY-20261023 — 2026-10-23 — completed

- **Owner / scope:** FIX-01/Sentinel source-error remediation directly on `Development`; start from previous completed email-action reply implementation, re-read `docs/project-control/CHAT_CONTINUITY.md`, `NEXT_ACTION.md`, shared AGENT_MEMORY and FIX-01 memory, relevant central agent records, current issues and prior worklog. DEV-01 has independent provider setup/product changes, OPS-01 owns deployment/CI. Only this root `worklog.md` changed among Markdown files. Phase 4 remains in progress; no standing delivery/routine/WhatsApp permissions activated. No live user session, email content, secret or mailbox accessed.
- **Reproducible RED symptom:** In `apps/web/src/app/api/chat/stream/route.ts`, the successful *registered, audited read-only tool* branch iterated `claraRuntime.streamAuthorizedReadOnlyToolAnswer` and buffered all provider tokens, then called `userFacingChatAnswer(generated)` and returned `{ text, streamed: true }` from the host tool loop **without a single `emit("delta", ...)`**. The caller's `if (!toolTurn.streamed)` guard suppressed a second emit; browser received completion without an assistant answer even though the answer was saved in the conversation. In Voice, the equivalent read-only final streamed raw untrusted provider tokens before checking for internal model planning, potentially speaking private planning; its normal save path relied on emitted text. Both problems came from an incomplete prior safety/streaming integration. Regression authored **before** correction as `apps/web/tests/chat-voice-readonly-final-delivery.test.mjs` (commit `441319357a89821c59ffa739c90ecd07949c8563`).
- **Minimal correction:** Chat `7f0763e57461811068fd4acc3c0a77623eb8b4b4` emits the fully validated host-derived answer **once** before reporting it already streamed; preserves no model-token exposure before validation and honors aborted HTTP request. Voice `3eee398c910a17e8f9fa5c410e553d6dc972d6c5` buffers the read-only model answer, reuses `userFacingChatAnswer` before emitting, and returns only the validated text, preventing raw private-planning audio. Voice abort and empty-answer handling remain fail-closed; `deliveredText` now reflects exactly what can be persisted and spoken. Repaired an abort-aware voice test assertion in `5e05b290edaa771607388fa75c13076819487bf7`. Updated the existing `apps/web/tests/verified-tool-streaming-contract.test.ts` assertion in `b45c4031dcedf367ba6e51e5b309127eec91000a` to expect validated Chat/Voice text, not obsolete unverified `result.text` emission. The regular non-tool live token stream, bounded host tool executor, agent binding, authentication, actor `email.write`, draft review and human send approval are untouched.
- **Failed/unsafe approaches avoided:** Do not mark a callback's return as `streamed` when nothing was emitted; do not simply remove the `!toolTurn.streamed` guard because it duplicates responses for genuinely streamed paths; do not stream raw Voice tokens before internal-planning validation; do not solve missing write permission by elevating the user's role or sending through a provider; do not trust an LLM's unverified send claims.
- **Evidence and limits:** Re-fetched current GitHub `Development` source and both affected test files after commits; **18/18 targeted source invariants PASS**, covering exact one-time post-validation delivery in Chat/Voice, abort behavior, no early voice model-token leakage, preserved final answer/persistence, registered tool/permission safeguards, and four new Node regression cases. This is source inspection and newly committed tests only: **committed Node suite, full Vitest, worker/web typecheck/build, authenticated browser, PostgreSQL conversation persistence, real model/tool/voice transport, production rollout, GitHub Actions and Vercel were NOT RUN** in the connector-only assistant environment. Voice read-only final SSE text is intentionally delayed until complete-answer safety validation; no unsupported low-latency streaming claim is made.
- **Prevention lesson:** For a validated buffer-to-stream path, the component setting `streamed=true` must itself deliver a user-facing delta **after** validation and **before** done; track displayed text and persisted voice text consistently. Keep untrusted planning/private provider text out of user-visible Chat, Voice and speech synthesis even if the provider streams tokens.
- **Exact next acceptance (no CI/Vercel waiting):** At a fresh sanitized `Development` SHA run `node --experimental-strip-types --test apps/web/tests/chat-voice-readonly-final-delivery.test.mjs apps/web/tests/chat-voice-email-action-feedback.test.mjs`, `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web exec vitest run tests/verified-tool-streaming-contract.test.ts tests/chat-host-tool-loop.test.ts tests/streaming-conversations.test.ts tests/voice-email-tool-runtime.test.ts`, then `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web typecheck && pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/worker build`. In an authorized disposable browser/DB, ask the chat agent to *read* a bound mailbox with `email.read`: verify a nonempty SSE delta before done, persisted assistant reply and no internal planning. For approved Voice read-only turns, verify validated text is spoken exactly once; empty/private planning triggers error rather than a false completed answer. Run denied send request as a separate `email.write` negative and confirm zero draft/approval/sends. ### FIX01-INBOX-SCOPED-PAGINATION-20261023 — Inbox 500-row contract and folder-scoped visibility
- **Role/authorization and recovery:** Owner directed FIX-01 / Sentinel, independent implementation **error fixing only** on `Rilan-Dev/ai-comms-agent` `Development`, without waiting on CI/Vercel/Phase gate and without editing any `.md` except the append-only root `worklog.md`. Recovered latest start HEAD `f8bfc5189da0b3a0f500522a96745d6b38753fb2`, `AGENTS.md`, `docs/project-control/CHAT_CONTINUITY.md`, `NEXT_ACTION.md`, shared `AGENT_MEMORY.md`, FIX-01 memory/worklog, agent sessions, issues and verification. Phase 4 stays `implementation_in_progress`; DEV-01 simultaneously owns provider/compose/catch-up UI, OPS-01 owns deployment/CI/infrastructure. No program features, migrations, credentials, account policies or external delivery altered.
- **Newly reproduced independent source defect:** The public Inbox API (`apps/web/src/app/api/mail/inbox/route.ts`) explicitly validates `limit` from 1 through **500** and returns that requested limit, but `packages/db/src/email/repository.ts` `listInboxMessages` silently clamps to **100**. Example `limit=500&offset=500` reached the database as `LIMIT 100 OFFSET 500`, skipping up to 400 rows when a client advances pages using the advertised limit. The repository also unconditionally included non-IMAP messages from non-Inbox folders via `a.provider <> 'imap_smtp' OR EXISTS (... Inbox ...)` and lacked `m.is_draft=false`. A newly saved Chat draft is a real local `email_messages` row with `is_draft=true`, `folder_id=NULL`; it could consequently appear in the Inbox for Gmail/Zoho and similar non-IMAP accounts rather than the Drafts view. All-message query also lacked a matching `m.organization_id = $1` predicate (despite matching account org), and equal timestamps had no deterministic tie-breaker. This is source-reproduced correctness, not proof of a particular live inbox incident.
- **Test-first RED:** Created `apps/web/tests/mail-inbox-scoped-pagination.test.mjs` on Development, commit `a5c67cd0e62427ed28dd8047de054f511f9af26f`. The test extracts the **actual** repository Inbox query body, removes only two TypeScript-only annotations, evaluates it using a mock SQL query sink, and checks route limit 500, DB params, both tenant predicates, draft exclusion, positive folder semantics for mapped IMAP/other providers, conservative unmapped non-IMAP inbound fallback, preserved filters and stable pagination order. Running the pre-fix GitHub source in the same extracted-body harness showed **0/5** core invariants satisfied: limit500, same-org message predicate, is_draft exclusion, positive folder classification and deterministic order. The actual query bound `[100,500]` for a request of `[500,500]`.
- **Minimal source correction / GREEN:** Commit `7d4f1c2fb1523880d37eeb61c90e6f103926f00e` modifies **only** `listInboxMessages` in `packages/db/src/email/repository.ts`: enforce `m.organization_id = $1` as well as the existing account tenant scope, `m.is_draft = false`, and positive Inbox folder membership for ALL mapped provider folders. Historical non-IMAP inbound rows without a resolved folder remain visible only if `m.folder_id IS NULL AND m.is_sent=false`; unmapped sent messages and all local drafts are excluded. Existing IMAP `folder:UID` duplicates continue to require true INBOX membership, with no destructive dedup or sync modification. Raise DB maximum to **500** to match API, retain default 50, minimum 1 and existing offset/account/unread/query/action filters. Add `m.id DESC` as deterministic tie-breaker behind received-at sort. No table changes or send/provider calls.
- **Verification:** Executed extracted committed GitHub repository query builder through isolated V8 with a mocked async database query, ensuring the **real query/parameter construction** is exercised. Post-fix **12/12** targeted checks PASS, including `LIMIT 500 OFFSET 500`, tenant filters, draft exclusion, mapped Inbox checks, unmapped inbound-only fallback, all existing query filters and stable ordering. Separately executed **all five committed .mjs test callbacks** after mocking only Node file reads/assertions in V8 against exact current GitHub repo+API source: **5/5 PASS**. Original 5-source-invariant RED and 100/500 parameter mismatch were observed before the fix. This is stronger than string checks alone but is **NOT a live PostgreSQL integration, native repository Node/Vitest, TypeScript build, authenticated browser, actual mailbox contents, CI/Vercel or production verification**. Transaction/index/execution-plan behavior and provider-specific folder fixtures remain to be exercised in disposable PostgreSQL before rollout acceptance.
- **Failed approaches avoided:** Did not delete imported IMAP duplicates, infer folder membership from from_email, equate all outbound with non-Inbox when explicitly mapped to INBOX (sent-to-self), mark a local draft sent, globally reduce Gmail sync to Inbox, remove account/tenant filters, disable user permission checks, expose body content in Inbox list, modify app feature work or silently change the external API pagination contract. The conservative null-folder fallback preserves legacy non-IMAP inbound but cannot prove membership when folder metadata was never imported.
- **Prevention / next action:** A public paging maximum and underlying query maximum must match. Inbox membership should derive from resolved folder classification and draft state, not the provider name; deterministic tie ordering protects offset navigation. On a sanitized dependency-equipped `Development` checkout run `node --test apps/web/tests/mail-inbox-scoped-pagination.test.mjs`, `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web exec vitest run tests/mail-imap-folder-copies.test.ts tests/mail-read-permission-boundary.test.ts` and `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/db typecheck && pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web typecheck`. In disposable PostgreSQL seed 501+ inbound records, non-IMAP local draft, mapped Sent and Inbox folder, unmatched legacy inbound/outbound, IMAP Inbox/All-Mail copies and equal timestamps; verify page 1/page 2 continuity and org A/B denial. OPS-01 owns any deployed SHA, schema/provider runtime testing and CI/Vercel; DEV-01 continues independent features. Continue FIX-01 with a different reproducible bug; do not hold for external gate.

### FIX01-MIGRATION-TEST-TS18048-20261023 — Lockfile document narrowing under strict TypeScript
- **Owner/scope:** FIX-01 / Sentinel, direct `Development` error-only correction. User's sanitized equipped host pulled `Development` from `1131776e` to `7bfdd35e` and ran six chat/email tests: **28/28 PASS**. `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web typecheck` then **FAILED** with only two TS18048 diagnostics in `apps/web/tests/migration-verification.test.ts` lines 735–736. Independently recovered current GitHub `Development` at `2d1e5ae3ab7c2e77ac2b880647fba40b9729ba78`, read-only `AGENTS.md`, `docs/project-control/CHAT_CONTINUITY.md`, `NEXT_ACTION.md`, role FIX-01 memory/worklog and exact test file. No unrelated feature work, CI/Vercel polling, PR, branch switch, production config, dependency update or policy changes.
- **Symptom/root cause:** The pnpm lockfile-integrity test splits `pnpm-lock.yaml` into `docs: string[]`, asserts `expect(docs).toHaveLength(2)`, then destructures `const [manager, workspace] = docs`. Workspace `noUncheckedIndexedAccess` still types both variables as `string | undefined`; Vitest's `toHaveLength(2)` does **not** narrow the array type for TypeScript. Subsequent `manager.match(...)` and `workspace.match(...)` generate `TS18048`, even though runtime tests may pass. No lockfile format/runtime defect established by this compiler error.
- **Reproduction / RED:** Used local Node v22 and globally available `tsc` (no dependency-equipped private checkout) to compile a faithful minimal snippet of the destructure+`expect`+`match` under `--strict --noUncheckedIndexedAccess --noEmit`; produced **exactly 2 TS18048** errors, one for each value, and nonzero exit. The user already supplied the same two errors from real `pnpm` web typecheck, with 28/28 chat/email tests green.
- **Remedy:** Single focused source commit `a22662ebc6fc7601a6e104c2543e47d74e75a522` adds an explicit `if (manager === undefined || workspace === undefined) throw new Error(...)` immediately after destructuring. Malformed/single-document lockfiles now fail early with a clear runtime exception rather than accessing undefined; the existing `docs` length assertion, pnpm 12 version/integrity assertions, per-document `importers` regex assertions and dependency snapshot tests remain **unchanged**. No non-test implementation code or `pnpm-lock.yaml` modified.
- **GREEN evidence:** The same isolated `tsc --strict --noUncheckedIndexedAccess` repro compiled cleanly after inserting the identical guard (exit 0). Re-fetched GitHub `Development` committed test and validated **6/6** invariants (two-doc assertion, explicit guard/message, both original regex checks, additional workspace check); branch had advanced concurrently to `9342a2bb` during verification, but the committed test guard remained intact. **NOT RUN by FIX-01**: actual installed-repo `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web typecheck`, exact migration Vitest, full test suite, package build, CI/Vercel or deployment. The user's 28/28 green tests predated this change and are not presented as post-fix acceptance.
- **Failed/unsafe approaches avoided:** Did not use non-null assertions (`manager!`), `as string`, `@ts-ignore`, `skipLibCheck` or weakening `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`, delete the lockfile assertions, downgrade TypeScript, edit the lockfile, or intervene in concurrent DEV-01 code. No Markdown changed other than this append-only root `worklog.md`.
- **Prevention/next action:** Framework matchers assert at runtime but do not inherently narrow array/tuple types. When code relies on an array index, add a real TypeScript-observable guard (or a typed length assertion) before calling string methods, preserving fail-fast coverage. On the user's equipped current-Development checkout run `git pull --ff-only origin Development`, then `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web typecheck` and `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web exec vitest run tests/migration-verification.test.ts`; report any different compiler failures as distinct FIX-01 defects. Continue independently fixing other reproducible implementation errors without waiting for GitHub Actions, Vercel or gates. OPS-01 owns host, dependency-install, CI and deployment defects.

- **New independently reproducible source symptom:** Even after Gmail IMAP folder duplicate display correction and canonical knowledge-index dedup, the chat assistant can consume two physically distinct `INBOX` / `[Gmail]/All Mail` rows of one RFC822 message in its 20-row evidence context. The Chat SSE `loadEmailContext()` read from `email_messages` directly, and Clara's `groundContext()` secondarily queried the same raw table; neither ranked the copies, though both correctly scoped by agent read-only mailbox bindings. Duplicate records could inflate apparent message counts, displace other emails from `LIMIT 20` and produce repeated AI answers. - **RED / root cause:** Existing `cce92f98/0cc16a77` fixed Inbox/Threads SQL read projection only. Existing knowledge canonicalizer protects derived RAG documents and vector jobs, not either direct Chat/Clara raw-row retrieval. Prior queries had no `internet_message_id` grouping or canonical folder choice, and `LIMIT 20` applied before any dedup. Regression created **first** at `fbbfefd1`: six native Node source-contract cases for both retrieval paths, preserving tenant+agent read binding, inbox priority, case-insensitive nonblank RFC822 header, separate-account/provider/blank-ID behavior and exact search/limit ordering. The pre-fix source plainly lacked ROW_NUMBER and alias-scoped dedup; test was committed before implementation (not actually run under host).
- **Corrective source:** `9cbc820a` changes only the ambient Chat email context query to CTE `ranked` with `ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY email_account_id, CASE provider='imap_smtp' AND nonblank internet_message_id THEN lower(trim(message_id)) ELSE message-row-UUID END)`. Chooses INBOX by folder role/provider name with deterministic received-time/ID tie, applies existing parameterized query filters and authenticated agent binding inside the CTE before ranking; emits only `duplicate_rank=1` before the bounded 20-row evidence limit. `9ff22021` applies the same logic to Clara's direct email `groundContext` retrieval while preserving its existing `$1` org, `$2` agent, `$3` text search, `$4` lookup terms, output metadata and separate content chunk retrieval. No mailbox rows, vector documents, account grants, HTTP API or prompt authority changed. Regression adjustments `f9c0147f` (assert full-text fragment in actual builder) and `34c8c9eb` (assert interpolated Chat binding at its definition rather than query text).
- **Failed/unsafe alternatives avoided:** No deleting/merging physical provider folder rows or globally grouping by subject/time/content; no bypass of `agent_email_accounts` readable roles, organization boundaries or source filters; no dedup **after** `LIMIT 20`; no disabling RAG, rewriting indexes, touching DEV-01's concurrent chat UI, enabling C3 routines, or changes to migrations/deploy. For blank Message-ID, different accounts and non-IMAP providers, original separate record identities remain.
- **Evidence / verification limits:** Re-fetched changed Chat/Clara/test source blobs and executed **37/37** direct structural checks in connected GitHub code environment: rank, key, idempotent tie, folder preference, account/tenant/agent guards, existing search terms, rank-before-limit and original regression retention. Six new `node:test` test cases are committed; **actual Node test runner, web/Clara TypeScript checks, PostgreSQL query planning/results, browser/LLM answers, Qdrant and production deployed SHA NOT RUN**. Static checks do not prove SQL runtime correctness or production acceptance.
- **Prevention lesson:** An IMAP `folder:UID` is a physical mailbox view, not an email identity. Read projections feeding the assistant must dedup with the same account-scoped logical RFC822 key as inbox and indexer, **before** pagination; otherwise AI still receives repeated evidence even if the UI and vectors look fixed. Keep authorization predicates *inside* the ranked CTE and avoid conflating model context counts with actual full inbox totals.
- **Exact next action:** On matching `Development` with dependencies installed, run `node --test apps/web/tests/chat-email-folder-copy-retrieval.test.mjs`, then `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web exec vitest run tests/chat-memory-regression.test.ts tests/chat-ambient-context-policy.test.ts`, `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web typecheck`, `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/clara-core typecheck`; if a focused query/TypeScript failure is reproduced, FIX-01 resolves it. OPS-01 owns pnpm `Unknown option: recursive` environment/command triage and safe disposable Postgres/production rollout. Both authorized workers (`apps/worker/src/email/action-worker.ts` and `action-executor.ts`) later resolve `snapshot.messageId` using `getMailMessageForOrganization(organizationId,messageId)`, which performs `WHERE m.id=$1` (UUID), and only **then** extracts `source.provider_message_id` for the external `connected.reply/forward` call. Existing tenant+actor permission, saved agent allowlist, approved snapshot, connected mailbox and human approval boundaries stay unchanged. External provider IDs are resolved ONLY after worker fetches the trusted local source.

### FIX01-MAIL-ACTIONS-NULLABLE-SOURCE-20261023 — Build TS18047 nullable source record
- **Role, branch, recovery and authorization:** Owner-directed FIX-01 / Sentinel independently correcting a pasted, reproducible Docker web build error on `Rilan-Dev/ai-comms-agent` branch `Development`. Starting branch HEAD `ece82ff8be88beee0db93795119ec7fd15a3404f`; inspected `AGENTS.md`, `docs/project-control/CHAT_CONTINUITY.md`, `docs/project-control/NEXT_ACTION.md`, FIX-01 local memory/worklog, the current mail-actions route, source identity helper, DB email message getter, and existing worker/web mail-approval tests. Project-control Markdown remains read-only. Root lowercase `worklog.md` is this FIX-01 turn's only Markdown edit. Phase 4 stays implementation-in-progress; DEV-01 feature scope and OPS-01 infrastructure/deployment untouched.
- **Observed failure:** Owner's `docker compose` web build compiled optimized assets but failed during Next.js typecheck at `apps/web/src/app/api/mail/actions/route.ts:95:12`, `Type error: 'message' is possibly 'null'` when reading `message.from_email ?? message.from_address` for a reply approval. This is concrete build evidence from the owner's working checkout, not a Vercel/CI gate or operational delivery failure.
- **Root cause:** `getMailMessageForOrganization(organizationId,messageId)` returns `Promise<Record<string,unknown>|null>`. The existing `eligibleReplyForwardSourceId(message,emailAccountId)` validates the same-org/provider-backed source but returns `string|null`, **not** a TypeScript type predicate. Consequently `if (!eligibleReplyForwardSourceId(message,emailAccountId)) return ...` did not narrow `message`, even though null returns a false result at runtime. Later sender and subject reads remained nullable to TypeScript.
- **RED regression / safe correction:** Regression-first commit `bca26b47b7766cf6a1a6a24390c58de2859c2008` extends `apps/worker/tests/reply-forward-approval-mailbox-safety.test.ts`: explicit null guard must precede accessing reply sender and subject; invalid/missing source must still fail closed. Direct `Development` source fix `f5b02ce2139b61871202832cef16115d55141627` changes **one guard only** to `if (!message || !eligibleReplyForwardSourceId(message, emailAccountId))` in `apps/web/src/app/api/mail/actions/route.ts`. This preserves all original tenant, mailbox, UUID/provider-backend, recipient validation, human review, and approval constraints. No provider calls, role change, schema change, send/grant, CI/Vercel polling, workflow activation or deployment.
- **Evidence and limits:** Against exact GitHub baseline/source, focused source checks went **3/6 → 6/6**: explicit null guard and ordering newly pass; mailbox eligibility, fail-closed 404 and strict recipient validation remain. Read back committed regression and corrected source GitHub blobs. Independently executed strict native TypeScript 5.8.3 `tsc --strict --noEmit --skipLibCheck --target es2022` on a minimal fixture reproducing the exact imported getter and helper return signatures and same header access: OLD exit 2 with three TS18047 nullable-object errors; guarded NEW exit 0. This proves TypeScript control-flow narrowing in isolation, **not** an equipped whole-web build or CI acceptance.
- **Failed/unsafe approaches avoided:** Did not assert nonnull via `message!` or `as Record<...>`, change helper's public signature, bypass same-organization eligibility, use fallback nullish strings for missing sender, delete regression tests, or turn off strictNullChecks. The failure cannot be fixed by waiting for GitHub Actions or Vercel. No other Markdown touched.
- **Prevention lesson/next:** Functions returning nonboolean evidence (ID or null) do not automatically narrow their argument. Always explicitly reject a nullable DB result before reading its fields, then apply provider/scope eligibility checks. Owner or OPS-01 may immediately rerun `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web build` in their existing development container; run targeted native `cd apps/worker && node --import tsx --test tests/reply-forward-approval-mailbox-safety.test.ts` and web `pnpm --filter @ai-comms-agent/web typecheck` on a clean matching `Development` checkout. If the next concrete compiler error appears, fix independently rather than polling build stages. OPS-01 owns Docker/Vercel/runtime environment, DEV-01 owns unrelated product development. Do not claim this is a successful full build until actually observed.


### FIX01-SUBJECTLESS-FORWARD-APPROVAL-20261023 — 2026-10-23 — FIX-01 / Sentinel

- **Additional reproduced source defect, same fully repaired AI reply/forward lifecycle:** `email.forward.request` previously created an approval using `subject: text(source.subject,998)`. For a legitimate received mail without a Subject header this persisted an **empty** reviewed subject. Both workers later call `paramsFromApprovedEmailSnapshot`, which rejects an empty subject *after* human approval. In contrast, manual approval and AI `draft_forward` already prefixed `Fwd:`. No production delivery, recipient or approval was inspected; this is an independently demonstrable source contract mismatch.
- **RED→GREEN:** Test-first regression `e26d6b990379f222dffebc1904c1818b02ae2447` in `apps/worker/tests/email-reply-forward-source-identity.test.mjs` asserts nonempty null/blank forwards, ordinary and already-prefixed subjects, a 998-character bound, and **both** draft+approval using one helper. Before fix helper was absent and `requestForward` still used raw subject. Pure helper `reviewedForwardSubject` committed `b17829b282110ece3c06dd01fccffc12b47536d5`, and `4f13dc204cc78b854c0d17ea4c3c52d9141ef9dd` binds it into draft and approval. Missing subjects become `Fwd: (no subject)`; normal subjects get one forward prefix and are safely length-limited.
- **Evidence:** Fresh GitHub source, worker, regression and approval-snapshot parser checks **9/9 PASS**. Copied the **exact committed helper** into local Node fixture; `git hash-object` matched GitHub blob SHA `b2520fd5373170cba4b086f50534d451bf6cfc42`. Isolated `node --experimental-strip-types --test` **6/6 PASS** and strict isolated `tsc --noEmit --skipLibCheck --strict --target ES2022 --module NodeNext --moduleResolution NodeNext` exited 0. No equipped repo native test, full workspace TS, PostgreSQL, actual approval or provider execution, CI/Vercel or deployed verification. This result does NOT mean an email was sent.
- **Concurrency preserved:** A concurrent agent updated root `worklog.md` while this addendum was being appended, producing a safe SHA conflict. Refetched live Development worklog instead of overwriting it. That agent independently corrected a nullable-message narrowing compile error in the manual reply/forward source guard, documented in its own appended evidence. The remaining source regression must run on the latest current SHA; do not infer full build pass from isolated TS checks.
- **Failure prevention / next:** Worker-time snapshot preconditions (including nonempty subject and correct local UUID) must be satisfied **before** presenting a send/reply/forward approval. No role grant, source migration, external send, changed human approval, provider operation, new feature or other Markdown was introduced. Run `node --experimental-strip-types --test apps/worker/tests/email-reply-forward-source-identity.test.mjs`, worker and web typechecks, and disposable tenant/account/provider vs local ID plus subjectless-forward fixtures on the exact current Development checkout.
